'''Neymar da Silva Santos Júnior'''{{efn|group=lower-greek|{{IPA|pt-br|nejˈmaʁ dɐ ˈsiwvɐ ˈsɐ̃tuz‿ˈʒuni.oʁ|audio=Neymar pronunciation.mp3}}<!--This is an IPA transcription based on the actual pronunciation and following [[MOS:PRON]] and [[H:IPA-PT]]. Please do not remove it.-->}} (born 5 February 1992), is a Brazilian professional [[Association football|footballer]] who plays as an [[attacking midfielder]] and a [[Forward (association football)|forward]] for the [[Campeonato Brasileiro Série A]] club [[Santos FC|Santos]], which he also [[Captain (association football)|captains]]. He is known for his dribbling, technical ability, agility, passing, and finishing. Often regarded as one of the greatest players of all time, Neymar is both the [[Brazil national football team#Top goalscorers|all-time top goalscorer]] ([[List of international goals scored by Neymar|80]]) and assist provider (59) for the [[Brazil national football team|Brazil national team]]. He is one of the few players to have scored over 100 goals with three different clubs.{{efn|group=lower-greek|The other four players are [[Isidro Lángara]], [[Romário]], [[Cristiano Ronaldo]], and [[Robert Lewandowski]].}} Official [[Opta Sports|performance data]] ranked him as one of the top two players in Europe for five consecutive seasons from 2015–16 to 2019–20, ranking as the best player in 2016–17 and 2017–18.  

Neymar made his professional debut with Santos in 2009 and soon emerged as a teenage sensation. In [[2011 Santos FC season|2011]], he led Santos to the [[2011 Copa Libertadores|Copa Libertadores]] title and was named the tournament's [[2011 Copa Libertadores#Best Player|best player]]. Neymar joined [[FC Barcelona|Barcelona]] in 2013, where he was part of the ''[[MSN (association football)|MSN]]'' attacking trio alongside [[Lionel Messi]] and [[Luis Suárez (Uruguayan footballer)|Luis Suárez]]. He finished as the joint [[List of UEFA Champions League top scorers|top scorer]] in the [[UEFA Champions League]] and the joint top scorer in the [[Copa del Rey]] as Barcelona completed the [[continental treble]] in [[2014–15 FC Barcelona season|2014–15]]. At the age of 23, Neymar was viewed as one of the world's three best players, alongside Messi and [[Cristiano Ronaldo]]. 

In 2017, Neymar joined [[Paris Saint-Germain FC|Paris Saint-Germain]] in a [[List of most expensive association football transfers|world-record]] €222 million transfer that made him one of the [[Forbes list of the world's highest-paid athletes|world's highest-paid sportsmen]]. However, his time at the club was largely affected by injuries. Despite this, he was particularly influential to [[Ligue 1]] title wins in [[2017–18 Ligue 1|2017–18]], [[2019–20 Ligue 1|2019–20]] and [[2022–23 Ligue 1|2022–23]], but finished as Champions League runners-up with PSG in [[2020 UEFA Champions League final|2020]]. In [[2020–21 UEFA Champions League|2020–21]], Neymar became the first player to score 20 goals in the Champions League with two different clubs. With 118 goals, he is PSG's [[List of Paris Saint-Germain FC records and statistics#Goalscorers|fourth-all-time top goalscorer]]. In 2023, he joined Saudi club [[Al Hilal SFC|Al-Hilal]] before returning to Santos in January 2025.

Neymar was [[Cap (sport)|capped]] 130 times for the Brazil national team, the [[Brazil national football team#Most appearances|second-most]] for the country. He appeared in four consecutive [[FIFA World Cup]]s from [[2014 FIFA World Cup|2014]] to [[2026 FIFA World Cup|2026]], scoring [[Brazil at the FIFA World Cup#Top goalscorers|nine goals]] in 15 matches. His international honours include the [[2013 FIFA Confederations Cup]], for which he won the [[Golden Ball (FIFA Confederations Cup)|Golden Ball]], as well as an [[Olympic medal|Olympic gold medal]] in [[Football at the 2016 Summer Olympics – Men's tournament|2016]] — the nation's first — and a silver medal in [[Football at the 2012 Summer Olympics|2012]]. After missing Brazil's triumph at the [[2019 Copa América]] due to injury, Neymar led them to the [[2021 Copa América final|final]] of the [[2021 Copa América]], for which he jointly received the [[2021 Copa América#Awards|Golden Ball]]. He retired from international football in 2026.

One of the world's most famous athletes, Neymar has been named the world's most marketable athlete by ''[[SportsPro]]'', has appeared regularly on ''[[Forbes]]''<nowiki/>' annual list of the world's highest-paid athletes, and was included in the [[Time (magazine)|''Time'']] [[Time 100|100 Most Influential People]] list in 2017.{{TOC limit|3}}

==Early life==
Neymar was born in [[Mogi das Cruzes]], [[São Paulo (state)|São Paulo]], to Neymar Santos Sr. and Nadine da Silva and had a [[Christians|Christian]] upbringing.<ref name="neymarjr">{{cite web|title=neymarjr|url=http://instagram.com/p/jgf7PgxtpQ/|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140728215425/http://instagram.com/p/jgf7PgxtpQ/|archive-date=28 July 2014}}</ref><ref name="instagram.com">{{cite web|title=neymarjr|url=http://instagram.com/p/jPcOdKRttJ/|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140728201305/http://instagram.com/p/jPcOdKRttJ/|archive-date=28 July 2014}}</ref> He inherited his name from his father, who is a former footballer and became his son's advisor as Neymar's talents began to grow.<ref>{{cite news | url = https://www.theage.com.au/sport/soccer/soccer-prodigy-neymar-is-at-home-in-brazil-20120710-21t7j.html | title = Soccer prodigy Neymar is at home in Brazil | work = The Age | access-date = 10 July 2012 | location = Melbourne | url-status=live | archive-url = https://web.archive.org/web/20131109123657/http://www.theage.com.au/sport/soccer/soccer-prodigy-neymar-is-at-home-in-brazil-20120710-21t7j.html | archive-date = 9 November 2013}}</ref> Neymar comments on his father's role: "My father has been by my side since I was little. He takes care of things, my finances and my family."<ref name="Samba Star">{{cite news |date=1 March 2012 |title=Work and play – Brazil's samba star Neymar has it all |url=https://edition.cnn.com/2012/03/01/sport/football/football-neymar-santos-brazil/index.html |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120409170138/http://edition.cnn.com/2012/03/01/sport/football/football-neymar-santos-brazil/index.html |archive-date=9 April 2012 |publisher=CNN}}</ref> Neymar has a very close [[sibling relationship]] with his sister Rafaella Beckran. He honoured Beckran by tattooing her face on his arm while she tattooed her brother's eyes on her arm.<ref>{{cite news|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150828230450/http://www.independent.co.uk/sport/football/news-and-comment/neymar-reveals-new-tattoo-of-his-sisters-face-on-his-arm-10345683.html|url=https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/football/news-and-comment/neymar-reveals-new-tattoo-of-his-sisters-face-on-his-arm-10345683.html|title=Neymar reveals new tattoo of his sister's face on his arm|archive-date=28 August 2015|work=The Independent|date=25 June 2015|access-date=1 August 2015}}</ref> 

Growing up, Neymar combined his love of [[futsal]] with [[Street football (association football)|street football]].<ref name="Back Page">{{cite web | url = http://backpagefootball.com/transfers/neymar-da-silva-santos-junior-the-world%E2%80%99s-hottest-property-2/ | title = Neymar da Silva Santos Júnior – The World's Hottest Property? | work = Back Page Football | url-status=live | archive-url = https://web.archive.org/web/20111117105022/http://backpagefootball.com/transfers/neymar-da-silva-santos-junior-the-world%E2%80%99s-hottest-property-2/ | archive-date = 17 November 2011}}</ref> Neymar said that futsal had a massive influence on him growing up, helping him develop his technique, speed of thought and ability to perform moves in tight spaces.<ref>{{Cite web|date=12 May 2020|title=Top 10 Pros Playing Today Who Started Off with Street Football and Futsal|url=https://urbanpitch.com/top-10-pros-playing-today-started-off-street-football/|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200813055959/https://urbanpitch.com/top-10-pros-playing-today-started-off-street-football/|archive-date=13 August 2020|access-date=15 January 2021 |website=FutsalFeed}}</ref> He was inspired by [[Robinho]] — his "idol" —  [[Ronaldinho]], [[Lionel Messi]] and [[Wayne Rooney]].<ref>{{cite web |date=18 January 2018 |title=Ronaldinho: The artist that inspired Messi and Neymar |url=https://www.marca.com/en/football/barcelona/2018/01/18/5a60a261ca4741ae388b458c.html |access-date=5 January 2025 |archive-date=27 December 2024 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20241227200541/https://www.marca.com/en/football/barcelona/2018/01/18/5a60a261ca4741ae388b458c.html |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>[http://www1.skysports.com/football/news/11833/7585054/neymar-cools-messi-comparisons "Neymar cools Messi comparison"] {{webarchive|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131210202838/http://www1.skysports.com/football/news/11833/7585054/neymar-cools-messi-comparisons|date=10 December 2013}}. Sky Sports. Retrieved 9 May 2014</ref><ref>{{Cite web |title=Neymar downplays claims he is the best Brazilian since Pele{{!}} All Football |url=https://m.allfootballapp.com/news/La-Liga/Neymar-downplays-claims-he-is-the-best-Brazilian-since-Pele/1214504 |access-date=4 August 2025 |website=AllfootballOfficial |archive-date=21 August 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250821161852/https://m.allfootballapp.com/news/La-Liga/Neymar-downplays-claims-he-is-the-best-Brazilian-since-Pele/1214504 |url-status=live }}</ref>

In 2003, Neymar moved with his family to [[São Vicente, São Paulo|São Vicente]], where he began playing for youth side [[Associação Atlética Portuguesa (Santos)|Portuguesa Santista]].<ref>{{cite news| url=http://m.globoesporte.globo.com/sp/santos-e-regiao/noticia/2012/11/atuacao-brilhante-pelo-sub-12-da-briosa-quase-fez-neymar-ir-para-rival.html| title=Performance by the under-12 Briosa almost did Neymar hit with São Paulo| date=7 January 2013| url-status=live| archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130928144251/http://m.globoesporte.globo.com/sp/santos-e-regiao/noticia/2012/11/atuacao-brilhante-pelo-sub-12-da-briosa-quase-fez-neymar-ir-para-rival.html| archive-date=28 September 2013}}</ref> Then, later in 2003, they moved to [[Santos, São Paulo|Santos]], where Neymar joined [[Santos FC|Santos]].<ref>{{cite news| url=http://soccertalk.us/soccer-news/the-trajectory-of-a-soccer-star-neymar/| title=The Trajectory of a Soccer Star – Neymar| date=7 January 2013| url-status=dead| archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130613084109/http://soccertalk.us/soccer-news/the-trajectory-of-a-soccer-star-neymar| archive-date=13 June 2013| access-date=7 January 2013}}</ref> With the success of his youth career and added income, the family bought their first property, a house next to Vila Belmiro, Santos' home stadium. Their quality of family life improved, as at age 15, Neymar was earning {{gaps|10|000}} [[Brazilian real|reais]] per month and at 16, {{gaps|125|000}} reais per month. At 17, he signed his first full professional contract, was upgraded to the Santos first team, and began signing his first [[sponsor (commercial)|sponsor]]ship deals.<ref name="Merca Foot">{{cite news |url=http://mercafoot.com/page/neymar-el-barcelona-piensa-en-su-fichaje-pero-podria-ser-sin-pep-guardiola-en-el-banquillo.html |title=Neymar: without Pep Guardiola on the Barce bench |work=Merca Foot |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html |archive-date=11 June 2015 }}</ref>

==Club career==
===Santos===
====Youth====
Neymar began playing football at an early age and he was soon spotted by [[Santos FC|Santos]] who offered him a contract in 2003; where he was inducted into their [[Football academy|youth academy]], which has, in the past, produced Brazilian internationals like [[Antônio Wilson Vieira Honório|Coutinho]], [[Clodoaldo]], [[Diego (footballer, born 1985)|Diego]], [[Elano]] and [[Alex (footballer, born June 1982)|Alex]]. He also joined the likes of [[José Macia|Pepe]], [[Pelé]] and Robinho in starting out his career at the club, nicknamed ''Peixe''.<ref name="Santos Player Profile">{{cite web|url=http://www.santosfc.com.br/futebol/elenco/jogador.asp?i=2|title=Atacantes – Neymar|publisher=Santos Futebol Clube|language=pt|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111214003655/http://www.santosfc.com.br/futebol/elenco/jogador.asp?i=2|archive-date=14 December 2011}}</ref> While in the youth academy, Neymar met [[Paulo Henrique Ganso]], becoming good friends in the process. Aged 14, Neymar travelled to Spain for tryouts with the [[Real Madrid C.F.|Real Madrid]] youth team. He did not stay in Madrid, however, as his father decided at the time that he preferred the young prodigy to keep growing up while playing at Santos.<ref>[https://www.nytimes.com/2012/07/10/sports/soccer/neymar-soccer-prodigy-is-at-home-in-brazil.html?pagewanted=all&_r=0 "Soccer Prodigy, at Home in Brazil"] {{webarchive|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170805062720/http://www.nytimes.com/2012/07/10/sports/soccer/neymar-soccer-prodigy-is-at-home-in-brazil.html?pagewanted=all&_r=0 |date=5 August 2017 }}. ''The New York Times''. Retrieved 22 May 2014</ref><ref>Neymar Sr.'s own words explaining the decision, as translated in the referred source: "We're from a humble family, and in a humble family there is always the question of cultural values," he said. "We thought he had to grow up in Brazil. That was the first serious choice we had to make."</ref>

==== 2009–11: Teenage sensation and Copa Libertadores triumph ====
{{Quote box|width=26%|align=right|quote="The 18-year-old is a magnificent prospect. He is sleek and skilful, able to beat the defender on either side, capable of combining well, and full of tricks he can put to productive use in and around the penalty area."|source=—South American football journalist [[Tim Vickery]] on Neymar in 2010.<ref>[https://www.bbc.co.uk/blogs/legacy/timvickery/2010/08/neymar_hailed_for_premier_leag.html "Neymar hailed for Premier League snub"] {{webarchive|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html |date=11 June 2015 }}. BBC Sport. Retrieved 14 January 2015</ref>}}
Neymar made his professional debut on 7 March 2009, one month after turning 17 years old, in a 2–1 win against [[Oeste Futebol Clube|Oeste]] in the [[2009 Campeonato Paulista]].<ref name="Back Page" /> On 11 April, Neymar scored the decisive goal in a 2–1 win against [[Sociedade Esportiva Palmeiras|Palmeiras]] in the [[Campeonato Paulista 2009#Semi-finals|2009 Campeonato Paulista semi-final first leg]].<ref>{{cite web|url=http://esportes.terra.com.br/futebol/estaduais/2009/interna/0,,OI3697654-EI12403,00-Neymar+da+vantagem+ao+Santos+no+duelo+com+Palmeiras.html|title=Neymar dá vantagem ao March 2012}}</ref> In the final, however, Santos suffered a 4–2 aggregate defeat to [[Sport Club Corinthians Paulista|Corinthians]].<ref>{{cite campeão paulista|date=3 May 2009 |publisher=Terra October 2012}}</ref> Neymar continued his rapid development in 2010, scoring 42 goals in 60 matches in all competitions and helping Santos win its first ever [[Copa do Brasil]] title, where he finished as the [[2010 Copa do Brasil|tournament's]] top scorer with 11 goals.<ref>{{cite web awash with hyperbole, but whatever happened to those compared with Brazil’s |title=The Next.... In the 2010 summer transfer window, Santos received bids for Neymar from [[Premier League]] clubs [[Chelsea F.C.|Chelsea]] and [[West Ham United F.C.|West Ham United]], the former reported to be in the region of £20&nbsp;million.<ref>{{Cite web|date=1 July 2010|title=West Ham chasing Brazilian Neymar |url=https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/football/teams/west-ham/7867750/West-Ham-chasing-Brazilian-Neymar.html|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201109015311/https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/football/teams/west-ham/7867750/West-Ham-chasing-Brazilian-Neymar.html|archive-date=9 November 2020|access-date=14 January 2021 |website=The Daily Telegraph}}</ref><ref name="chelseachase">[http://www.skysports.com/story/0,19528,11668_6313845,00.html Chelsea continue Neymar chase] {{webarchive|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20100817141919/http://www.skysports.com/story/0%2C19528%2C11668_6313845%2C00.html |date=17 August 2010 }} Sky Sports, 15 August 2010</ref> Neymar remained at Santos, but said a year later that it was a "dream" of his "to play in Europe", while also stating that at the time it had been the right decision to stay in Brazil.<ref name="chelseachase" /><ref>[http://www.caughtoffside.com/2011/05/19/brazilian-starlet-neymar-makes-it-clear-he-wants-chelsea-move/ Brazilian Starlet Neymar Makes It Clear He Wants Chelsea Move] {{webarchive|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110830151005/http://www.caughtoffside.com/2011/05/19/brazilian-starlet-neymar-makes-it-clear-he-wants-chelsea-move/ |date=30 August 2011 }} caughtoffside</ref>
[[File:Messi with Neymar Junior the Future of Brazil.jpg|thumb|Neymar and [[Lionel Messi]] greeting each other after the [[2011 FIFA Club World Cup final]]|left]]
Neymar scored crucial goals during the [[2011 Santos FC season|2011 season]], including the winning goal in the second leg of the finals against [[SC Corinthians Paulista|Corinthians]] to help his club clinch back-to-back state titles.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Barros |first=Adilson |date=15 May 2011 |title=Com direito a drama no fim, Peixe conquista o bi em cima do Timão |url=https://ge.globo.com/sp/futebol/campeonato-paulista/noticia/2011/05/com-direito-drama-no-fim-peixe-conquista-o-bi-em-cima-do-timao.html |access-date=12 February 2025 |website=globoesporte.com |archive-date=16 April 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250416151829/https://ge.globo.com/sp/futebol/campeonato-paulista/noticia/2011/05/com-direito-drama-no-fim-peixe-conquista-o-bi-em-cima-do-timao.html |url-status=live }}</ref> He scored six goals during Santos' run to the [[2011 Copa Libertadores finals]], including the opening goal in the second leg of the final against Uruguayan side [[C.A. Peñarol|Peñarol]], where Santos won 2–1. This was the club's first [[Copa Libertadores]] title since the "Pelé era" in the 1960s.<ref>{{Cite web|url=http://df1.conmebol.com/libertadores/fichas/ficha102833.html|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110909145558/http://df1.conmebol.com/libertadores/fichas/ficha102833.html|url-status=dead|title=Ficha del partido|archive-date=9 September 2011}}</ref><ref>{{cite web |url=http://msn.foxsports.com/fse/libertadores/stats |title=Estadísticas Copa Santander Libertadores – Información de equipos al web|url=http://df1.conmebol.com/libertadores/fichas/ficha102836.html|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110909073827/http://df1.conmebol.com/libertadores/fichas/ficha102836.html|url-status=dead|title=Ficha del partido|archive-date=9 September 2011}}</ref><ref>{{cite web |url=http://www.goal.com/en-gb/match/60013/santos-futebol-clube-vs-pe%C3%B1arol/report |title=Santos take home Copa Libertadores crown |work=Goal |access-date=12 March 2013 |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121015121802/http://www.goal.com/en-gb/match/60013/santos-futebol-clube-vs-pe%C3%B1arol/report |archive-date=15 October 2012 }}</ref> On 27 July 2011, he scored a spectacular solo dribble in a thrilling 5–4 loss against Ronaldinho's [[CR Flamengo|Flamengo]] that went on to be awarded the 2011 [[FIFA Puskás Award]].<ref>{{Cite web |date=27 July 2011 |title=Uma ode ao futebol: Flamengo vence Santos num jogo espetacular na Vila |url=https://ge.globo.com/futebol/brasileirao-serie-a/noticia/2011/07/uma-ode-ao-futebol-flamengo-vence-santos-num-jogo-espetacular-na-vila.html |access-date=12 February 2025 |website=globoesporte.com |url-status=live }}</ref> Later in the same year, he signed a contract extension until after the [[2014 FIFA World Cup]], reportedly increasing his wages by 50%, shunning off interest from top European clubs, including [[Real Madrid CF|Real Madrid]].<ref>{{cite news|title=Santos president warns Real Madrid over Neymar deal On 14 December, Neymar scored the opening goal from 20 meters for Santos as they defeated [[Kashiwa Reysol]] 3–1 in the semi-final of the [[2011 FIFA Club World Cup|FIFA Club World Cup]] in [[Toyota, Aichi|Toyota]], Japan.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Santos dance to final |url=https://www.aljazeera.com/sports/2011/12/14/santos-dance-into-club-world-cup-final |access-date=9 July 2025 |publisher=Al }}</ref><ref>{{Cite news |date=14 December 2011 |title=Neymar propels Santos into club world final |url=https://www.reuters.com/article/sports/neymar-propels-santos-into-club-world-final-idUSDEE7BD0F5/ |access-date=9 July 2025 |work=Reuters}}</ref> In the final on 18 December against [[FC Barcelona|Barcelona]], Neymar made a very small impact and Santos were defeated 4–0.<ref>{{cite web |url=http://soccernet.espn.go.com/report?id=334214&cc=5901 |title=Report: Kashiwa Reysol v Santos FC – FIFA Club World Cup – ESPN Soccernet |publisher=ESPN FC |date=14 December 2011 |access-date=12 March 2013 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html |archive-date=11 June 2015 }}</ref><ref name=":63">{{Cite news |date=18 December 2011 |title=Barcelona win Fifa Club World Cup |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/16234168 |access-date=9 July 2025 |work=BBC Sport |archive-date=21 May 2024 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240521124243/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/16234168 |url-status=live }}</ref> He was awarded the tournament's [[FIFA Club World Cup awards#Golden Ball|Bronze Ball]] as the third-best player, behind Messi and [[Xavi (footballer, born 1980)|Xavi]].<ref>{{Cite web |date=7 March 2019 |title=Neymar: 10 moments in 10 years |url=https://www.marca.com/en/football/international-football/2019/03/07/5c81920b468aeb49588b4573.htmll |access-date=9 July 2025 |website=MARCA in English}}</ref> On 31 December, he won the [[2011 South American Footballer of the Year]] award for the first time, by a record margin.<ref>[http://www.espnfc.com/blog/_/name/fiveaside/id/1099?cc=5739 "Neymar is Barca's latest Brazilian star"] {{webarchive|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140523011407/http://www.espnfc.com/blog/_/name/fiveaside/id/1099?cc=5739 |date=23 May 2014 }}. ESPN. Retrieved 22 May 2014</ref> In a [[Universo Online|UOL]] survey of 100 [[Campeonato Brasileiro Série A]] players, Neymar was voted the best player in Brazil, earning 89% of the votes.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Com 89%, Neymar é eleito pelos jogadores como o melhor do país em pesquisa do UOL Esporte |url=https://www.uol.com.br/esporte/futebol/ultimas-noticias/2012/12/24/com-89-neymar-e-eleito-pelos-companheiros-como-o-melhor-do-pais-em-pesquisa-do-uol.htm |access-date=12 July 2026 |website=www.uol.com.br |language=pt-br |archive-date=13 July 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260713045441/https://www.uol.com.br/esporte/futebol/ultimas-noticias/2012/12/24/com-89-neymar-e-eleito-pelos-companheiros-como-o-melhor-do-pais-em-pesquisa-do-uol.htm |url-status=live }}</ref>

==== 2012–13: Continued individual dominance and final season ====
[[File:Neymar ney (cropped) version 2.jpg|thumb|288x288px|Neymar (pictured in 2012) was awarded the [[2012 South American Footballer of the Year|South American Footballer of the Year]] for the second consecutive time.]]
The [[2012 Santos FC season|2012 season]] was arguably Neymar's finest with Santos. Early in the year, at 19 years old, Neymar finished 10th in the [[2011 FIFA Ballon d'Or]] rankings. This was an unprecedented feat for a non-European club player.<ref>{{Cite web|date=28 October 2024|title=Has Neymar ever won the Ballon d'Or? Brazil forward's all-time career results, finishes in award race {{!}} Sporting News United Kingdom|url=https://www.sportingnews.com/uk/football/news/has-neymar-won-ballon-dor-all-time-career-results-finishes-award/6f7ed3d561ed7f818fb467a6|access-date=13 June 2025|website=www.sportingnews.com|archive-date=4 October 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20251004111614/https://www.sportingnews.com/uk/football/news/has-neymar-won-ballon-dor-all-time-career-results-finishes-award/6f7ed3d561ed7f818fb467a6|url-status=live}}</ref> On 5 February 2012, when he turned 20, he scored his 100th goal as a professional football player, against Palmeiras in the [[2012 Campeonato Paulista|Campeonato Paulista]].<ref>{{cite news|title=Neymar scores 100 career goals, gets abused with cake ingredients|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/blogs/soccer-dirty-tackle/neymar-scores-100-career-goals-gets-abused-cake-192326497.html|access-date=3 March 2012|newspaper=Yahoo! Sports|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120213120203/http://sports.yahoo.com/blogs/soccer-dirty-tackle/neymar-scores-100-career-goals-gets-abused-cake-192326497.html|archive-date=13 February 2012}}</ref><ref>{{cite news|date=5 February 2012 |title=Neymar scores 100th career goal in Brazil |url=http://sportsillustrated.cnn.com/soccer/news/20120205/neymar-brazil/ |access-date=3 March 2012 |newspaper=Sports Illustrated|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120420234538/http://sportsillustrated.cnn.com/soccer/news/20120205/neymar-brazil/|archive-date=20 April 2012}}</ref> On 25 February, he scored two goals, one of which was from 25-yards, and created two assists to help his side to a 6–1 win over [[Associação Atlética Ponte Preta|Ponte Preta]].<ref>{{cite news|title=Santos FC 6 – 1 AA Ponte Preta|url=http://soccernet.espn.go.com/match?id=336661&cc=3888|access-date=25 February 2012|publisher=ESPN|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html|archive-date=11 June 2015}}</ref> On 7 March, Neymar netted a [[Hat-trick (association football)|hat-trick]] as Santos saw off Brazilian rivals [[Sport Club Internacional|Internacional]] with a 3–1 win in the [[2012 Copa Libertadores|Copa Libertadores]] group stage.<ref>{{cite news|title=Neymar treble lifts Santos to victory|url=https://www.fifa.com/worldfootball/clubfootball/news/newsid=1596364.html|access-date=8 March 2012|publisher=FIFA|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120514051426/http://www.fifa.com/worldfootball/clubfootball/news/newsid%3D1596364.html|archive-date=14 May 2012}}</ref> In the fixture against [[São Paulo FC|São Paulo]] on 29 April, he scored a hat-trick with the match ending 3–1.<ref>{{cite news|title=São Paulo 1 – 3 Santos FC|url=http://soccernet.espn.go.com/match?id=341105&cc=3888|access-date=29 April 2012|publisher=ESPN|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html|archive-date=11 June 2015}}</ref>

In the [[2012 Campeonato Paulista knockout phase#Finals|2012 Campeonato Paulista finals]] against [[Guarani FC|Guarani]], Neymar scored twice in the [[2012 Campeonato Paulista knockout phase#First leg|first]] and [[2012 Campeonato Paulista knockout phase#Second leg|second legs]], which ended 7–2 on aggregate to crown Santos champions.<ref>{{cite news|title=Guarani 0 – 3 Santos FC |url=http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/336762/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null |access-date=5 May 2012 |publisher=ESPN |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131231215557/http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/336762/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null |archive-date=31 December 2013 }}</ref><ref>{{cite news|title=Santos FC 4 – 2 Guarani |url=http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/336763/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null |access-date=13 May 2012 |publisher=ESPN |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131231215602/http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/336763/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null |archive-date=31 December 2013 }}</ref> He finished the [[2012 Campeonato Paulista]] with 20 goals and was voted the Best Player and Best Forward. He also finished as joint top scorer in the [[2012 Copa Libertadores|Copa Libertadores]] with eight goals, where Santos were eliminated by eventual champions Corinthians in the semi-finals. He was voted the Best Player of the [[2012 Recopa Sudamericana]], which Santos won.
[[File:NeymarAutographedCleats2012.jpg|left|thumb|Neymar's autographed boots from the [[2013 Santos FC season|2013 season]] on display at an exhibition ]]
On 25 August 2012, he scored a brace in the 2–1 away win at [[SE Palmeiras|Palmeiras]].<ref>{{cite news|title=Palmeiras 1 – 2 Santos FC|url=http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/341402/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null|access-date=25 August 2012|publisher=ESPN|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131231215642/http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/341402/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null|archive-date=31 December 2013}}</ref> On 3 November, in the [[2012 Campeonato Brasileiro Série A|league]] fixture away at [[Cruzeiro Esporte Clube|Cruzeiro]], Neymar scored a hat-trick and assisted [[Felipe Anderson]]'s goal, to help his side to a 4–0 win.<ref>{{cite news|title=Cruzeiro 0 – 4 Santos FC|url=http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/341555/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null|access-date=3 November 2012|publisher=ESPN|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131231215647/http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/341555/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null|archive-date=31 December 2013}}</ref> Neymar finished off the 2012 season in style, first setting-up [[Victor Andrade]]'s equaliser, then scoring twice, to give Santos a 3–1 home win over Palmeiras on 1 December.<ref>{{cite news|title=Santos FC 3 – 1 Palmeiras|url=http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/341592/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null|access-date=1 December 2012|publisher=ESPN|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121230174730/http://espnfc.com/en/gamecast/341592/gamecast.html?soccernet=true&cc=null|archive-date=30 December 2012}}</ref> Neymar finished the 2012 season with 43 goals in just 47 matches. He won the [[2012 South American Footballer of the Year]], retaining his award.<ref>{{cite web |url=http://www.ovaciondigital.com.uy/121231/futinter-685408/futbolinternacional/seleccion-ideal-de-america/ |trans-title=The best eleven of America |title=Selección Ideal de América |work=[[El País (Uruguay)|El País]] |date=31 December 2012 |access-date=7 April 2013 |archive-date=6 March 2013 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130306023719/http://www.ovaciondigital.com.uy/121231/futinter-685408/futbolinternacional/seleccion-ideal-de-america/ |url-status=dead }}</ref> Pelé claimed that Neymar is the best player in the world; former Argentine player [[Diego Maradona]] responded to this: "Maybe Neymar is the best player in the world, but only if you say that Messi is from a different planet."<ref>{{Cite news |last=Quraishi |first=George |date=9 June 2012 |title=Lionel Messi hat-trick leads Argentina to 4-3 win over Brazil |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2012/jun/10/argentina-brazil-lionel-messi-new-york-metlife |access-date=3 June 2025 |work=The Guardian |issn=0261-3077 |archive-date=21 September 2016 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160921210139/https://www.theguardian.com/football/2012/jun/10/argentina-brazil-lionel-messi-new-york-metlife |url-status=live }}</ref> At this point in his career, Neymar was touted to emulate Messi — or even surpass him.<ref>{{cite web |title=Ronaldo: Neymar can be better than Messi|url=http://www.givemesport.com/194801-ronaldo-neymar-can-be-better-than-messi|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140328103604/http://www.givemesport.com/194801-ronaldo-neymar-can-be-better-than-messi|archive-date=28 March 2014|access-date=30 June 2013|work=GiveMeSport}}</ref><ref>{{cite news |last=Green|first=Sam|date=19 May 2011|title=Next Big Thing|url=https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/football/teams/chelsea/8521834/Neymar-Brazils-Next-Big-Thing-could-still-be-the-answer-to-Chelseas-prayers.html|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120124192531/http://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/football/teams/chelsea/8521834/Neymar-Brazils-Next-Big-Thing-could-still-be-the-answer-to-Chelseas-prayers.html|archive-date=24 January 2012|work=The Daily Telegraph|location=London}}</ref><ref>{{cite web |date=5 February 2013|title=Ronaldinho: Neymar will emulate Messi|url=http://www.goal.com/en/news/584/brazil/2013/02/05/3729583/ronaldinho-tips-neymar-to-become-worlds-best|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130210030959/http://www.goal.com/en/news/584/brazil/2013/02/05/3729583/ronaldinho-tips-neymar-to-become-worlds-best|archive-date=10 Neymar started the [[2013 Campeonato Paulista]] scoring twice in the first match, which ended in a 3–1 win over [[São Bernardo Futebol Clube|São Bernardo]] on 19 January 2013.<ref>{{cite news|title=Sao Bernardo 1–3 Santos FC|url=http://soccernet.espn.go.com/match?id=360577&cc=3888|access-date=19 January 2013|publisher=ESPN|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html|archive-date=11 June 2015}}</ref> On 3 February, in the Paulista fixture against [[São Paulo FC|São Paulo]], Santos won 3–1 with Neymar scoring and making two assists.<ref>{{cite news|title=Santos FC 3–1 São Paulo|url=http://soccernet.espn.go.com/match?id=360619&cc=3888|access-date=3 February 2013|publisher=ESPN|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html|archive-date=11 June 2015}}</ref> Often compared to Pelé early in his career, he appeared on the cover of ''[[Time (magazine)|Time]]'' magazine with the title 'The Next Pelé' in February 2013.<ref>{{Cite magazine|last=Ghosh|first=Bobby|date=25 February 2013|title=A Q&A With Neymar, Brazil's Stay-At-Home Soccer Superstar|url=https://keepingscore.blogs.time.com/2013/02/25/a-qa-with-neymar-brazils-stay-at-home-soccer-superstar/|access-date=30 October 2023|magazine=Time|issn=0040-781X|archive-date=30 October 2023|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231030140201/https://keepingscore.blogs.time.com/2013/02/25/a-qa-with-neymar-brazils-stay-at-home-soccer-superstar/|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |last=Drehs |first=Wayne |title=Spotlight set to test legend of Neymar |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37371732/spotlight-set-test-legend-neymar |date=30 May 2014 |access-date=20 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN |archive-date=11 October 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20251011205713/https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37371732/spotlight-set-test-legend-neymar |url-status=live }}</ref> He scored all four goals as Santos beat [[União Agrícola Barbarense Futebol Clube|União Barbarense]] 4–0 in the [[Paulista]] on 13 April.<ref>{{cite news |title=Barca zero-in on Neymar who nets four|url=http://zeenews.india.com/sports/football/barca-zero-in-on-neymar-who-nets-four_759717.html|publisher=Zeenews|date=14 April 2013|access-date=18 May 2014|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140522142959/http://zeenews.india.com/sports/football/barca-zero-in-on-neymar-who-nets-four_759717.html|archive-date=22 May 2014}}</ref> On 25 April, his agent and father revealed that Neymar intended to leave for Europe before the 2014 World Cup.<ref>{{cite web |date=25 April 2013 |title=Neymar 'off to Europe after World Cup' |url=http://espnfc.com/news/story/_/id/1424891/neymar-europe-world-cup?cc=3436 |access-date=30 June 2013 |work=ESPN FC |url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131231215646/http://espnfc.com/news/story/_/id/1424891/neymar-europe-world-cup?cc=3436|archive-date=31 December 2013}}</ref> Neymar said the month prior that he had a "dream of playing in Europe, for a big club like Barcelona, Real Madrid and Chelsea."<ref>{{cite news|title=Neymar open to Chelsea move|url=http://www.espn.co.uk/football/sport/story/198275.html|access-date=18 March 2013|publisher=ESPN|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20141202203744/http://www.espn.co.uk/football/sport/story/198275.html|archive-date=2 December 2014}}</ref> Ahead of his last match for Santos, on 26 May against [[CR Flamengo|Flamengo]] in the first round of the [[2013 Campeonato Brasileiro Série A|2013 Brazilian Championship]], Neymar was in tears during the national anthem, with the match ending in a draw.<ref>{{cite web |title=Brazil says goodbye to Neymar, the 21-year-old who outgrew his home |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/snap-kaka-pop-blog/2013/may/31/brazil-goodbye-neymar |access-date=18 May 2014 |first=Jack|last=Lang|work=Guardian Sport Network|date=31 May 2014|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140522142021/http://www.theguardian.com/football/snap-kaka-pop-blog/2013/may/31/brazil-goodbye-neymar|archive-date=22 May 2014}}</ref>

In 2021, UOL included Neymar in their ranking of the 'Top 5 Greatest Players in the History of Santos'.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Milton Neves - Top-5: os maiores jogadores da riquíssima história do Santos |url=https://blogmiltonneves.uol.com.br/blog/2021/04/10/top-5-os-maiores-jogadores-da-riquissima-historia-do-santos/ |access-date=6 August 2026 |website=blogmiltonneves.uol.com.br |language=pt-br |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260807043222/https://blogmiltonneves.uol.com.br/blog/2021/04/10/top-5-os-maiores-jogadores-da-riquissima-historia-do-santos/ |archive-date=7 August 2026 |url-status=live}}</ref>

===Barcelona===
{{main|Neymargate}}
[[File:Neymar Barcelona presentation 1.jpg|thumb|289x289px|Neymar during his presentation as a [[FC Barcelona|Barcelona]] player on 3 June 2013]]
On 24 May 2013, Santos announced that they had received two offers for Neymar. The following day, Neymar announced he would sign with Barcelona on 27 May and join the team after playing in the [[2013 FIFA Confederations Cup]]. Neither Neymar nor the clubs released details on the transfer fee or personal terms, save to say he signed a five-year deal.<ref>{{Cite web|date=25 May 2013|title=Brazilian forward Neymar says he is joining Barcelona, will sign the contract on Monday|url=https://www.foxnews.com/sports/brazilian-forward-neymar-says-he-is-joining-barcelona-will-sign-the-contract-on-monday|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210114084903/https://www.foxnews.com/sports/brazilian-forward-neymar-says-he-is-joining-barcelona-will-sign-the-contract-on-monday|archive-date=14 January 2021|access-date=14 January 2021|publisher=Fox News}}</ref> On 3 June, Neymar was unveiled by Barcelona after passing medical tests and signing a contract that would keep him at the club through June 2018.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.mirror.co.uk/sport/football/transfer-news/barcelona-transfer-news-neymar-unveiled-1929711|title=Brazilian Neymar unveiled by Barcelona as striker completes transfer in five-year deal|date=3 June 2013|work=Mirror|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130808234049/http://www.mirror.co.uk/sport/football/transfer-news/barcelona-transfer-news-neymar-unveiled-1929711|archive-date=8 August 2013}}</ref>

Neymar was presented at the [[Camp Nou]] in front of 56,500 fans, a record turnout for a Brazilian player.<ref>{{cite web |author=FootballEspana&nbsp;–&nbsp;Tue, 4 June 2013 09:10 BST |url=http://uk.eurosport.yahoo.com/news/liga-neymar-presentation-draws-56-500-fans-081003011.html |title=Liga – Neymar presentation draws 56,500 fans – Yahoo Eurosport UK |publisher=Yahoo |date=4 June 2013 |access-date=19 March 2014 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131023205811/http://uk.eurosport.yahoo.com/news/liga-neymar-presentation-draws-56-500-fans-081003011.html |archive-date=23 October 2013 }}</ref> Club vice-president [[Josep Maria Bartomeu]] initially said Neymar's transfer fee was €57.1&nbsp;million and his release clause set at €190&nbsp;million.<ref>{{cite news |url=http://www.fcbarcelona.com/club/detail/article/detailed-figures-of-neymar-transfer |title=Detailed figures of Neymar transfer |publisher=FC Barcelona |date=24 January 2014 |access-date=25 May 2015 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150524082202/http://www.fcbarcelona.com/club/detail/article/detailed-figures-of-neymar-transfer |archive-date=24 May 2015 }}</ref><ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/020a-0e85073904d5-c99b88750864-1000--neymar-excited-by-messi-alliance/|date=3 June 2013|publisher=UEFA|title=Neymar excited by Messi alliance|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20141221113651/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D1959194.html|archive-date=21 December 2014}}</ref> Barcelona's doctor suggested he might need to gain weight to be able to cope physically in Spanish football.<ref>{{cite news |url=https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/football/european/barcelona-doctor-reveals-concerns-over-neymars-weight-8665661.html |title=Barcelona doctor reveals concerns over Neymar's weight |agency=Reuters |newspaper=The Independent |date=19 June 2013 |access-date=18 May 2014 |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140522142457/http://www.independent.co.uk/sport/football/european/barcelona-doctor-reveals-concerns-over-neymars-weight-8665661.html |archive-date=22 May 2014 }}</ref>

====Transfer investigation====
In January 2014, the prosecutor's office in [[Madrid]] began investigating the transfer fee that Barcelona paid for Neymar.<ref name="Staatsanwaltschaft untersucht Transfer von Neymar">{{cite news|title=Staatsanwaltschaft untersucht Transfer von Neymar|url=https://www.welt.de/sport/fussball/internationale-ligen/article123762443/Staatsanwaltschaft-untersucht-Transfer-von-Neymar.html|access-date=10 January 2014|newspaper=Die Welt|date=10 January 2014|language=de|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140110195356/http://www.welt.de/sport/fussball/internationale-ligen/article123762443/Staatsanwaltschaft-untersucht-Transfer-von-Neymar.html|archive-date=10 January 2014}}</ref> The documents submitted to the authorities on request contained contradictory information.<ref name="Staatsanwaltschaft untersucht Transfer von Neymar"/> On 23 January 2014, [[Sandro Rosell]] resigned from his position as president.<ref>{{cite news |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2014/jan/23/sandro-rosell-resigns-barcelona-president-neymar |title=Sandro Rosell resigns as president of Barcelona with immediate effect |date=23 January 2014 |access-date=27 September 2014 |work=The Guardian |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20141211041628/http://www.theguardian.com/football/2014/jan/23/sandro-rosell-resigns-barcelona-president-neymar |archive-date=11 December 2014 }}</ref> A day later, the details of the transfer were revealed by Barcelona; the transfer had in fact cost them €86.2&nbsp;million (£71.5&nbsp;million),<ref name="BBC Real">{{cite web|title=Barcelona: Neymar deal has damaged brand of La Liga club|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/0/football/26512767|work=BBC Sport|access-date=21 January 2016|date=10 March |url=http://www1.skysports.com/football/news/11833/9132103/primera-division-barcelona-reveal-details-of-deal-to-sign-brazil-star-neymar |title=Barcelona reveal details of deal to sign Brazil star Neymar |date=24 January 2014 |access-date=27 September 2014 |publisher=Sky Sports |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20141211041519/http://www1.skysports.com/football/news/11833/9132103/primera-division-barcelona-reveal-details-of-deal-to-sign-brazil-star-neymar |archive-date=11 December 2014 }}</ref><ref>{{cite news |url=http://www.espnfc.com/spanish-primera-divisi%C3%B3n/story/1691847/barcelona-details-neymars-862m-euro-deal |title=Barcelona offers Neymar }}</ref> with Neymar's parents confirmed to have received a €40&nbsp;million sum.<ref>{{cite news |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/25887952 |title=Barcelona paid Neymar's parents £34m for Brazil striker |date=24 January 2014 |access-date=27 September 2014 |work=BBC |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140127020255/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/0/football/25887952 |archive-date=27 January 2014 }}</ref><ref>{{cite news |last=Jenson |first=Pete |url=https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/football/news-and-comment/barcelonas-neymar-saga-you-want-to-sign-our-sonthat-will-be-40-million-euros-please-9085538.html |title=Barcelona's Neymar saga: You ====2013–14: Settling into Spain ====
[[File:Neymar v Almeria 020314 (cropped) 2.jpg|thumb|Neymar playing in a [[La Liga]] fixture against [[UD Almería|Almería]] in March 2014]]
Neymar made his official debut for Barcelona during the opening game of the [[2013–14 FC Barcelona season|2013–14]] [[2013–14 La Liga|La Liga]] season as a 63rd-minute substitute for [[Alexis Sánchez]] in a 7–0 win against [[Levante UD|Levante]].<ref>{{cite news |url=https://huffingtonpost.com/2013/08/18/barcelona-levante-messi-goals_n_3776882.html |title=Barcelona Routs Levante 7–0: Messi Scores Two Goals, Neymar Debuts |date=18 August 2013 |work=HuffPost |first=Chris |last=Greenberg |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130822065239/http://www.huffingtonpost.com/2013/08/18/barcelona-levante-messi-goals_n_3776882.html |archive-date=22 August 2013}}</ref> On 21 August, he played in the [[2013 Supercopa de España]] against [[2012–13 Copa del Rey|Copa del Rey]] holders [[Atlético Madrid]]; he made an immediate impact, scoring Barcelona's only goal as they won the Supercopa on away goals.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Atkinson |first=Tre' |title=Complete Analysis of Neymar's First 6 Months at FC Barcelona |url=https://bleacherreport.com/articles/1923692-fc-barcelona-complete-analysis-of-neymars-first-6-months-at-barcelona |access-date=13 June 2025 |website=bleacherreport.com |archive-date=7 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250707143527/https://bleacherreport.com/articles/1923692-fc-barcelona-complete-analysis-of-neymars-first-6-months-at-barcelona |url-status=live }}</ref>

On 18 September, he made his [[UEFA Champions League]] debut, [[Assist (football)|assisting]] a [[Gerard Piqué]] goal as Barça beat [[AFC Ajax|Ajax]] 4–0 in their opening match of the [[2013–14 UEFA Champions League|2013–14 tournament]]. Six days later, Neymar scored his first goal in La Liga in Barcelona's 4–1 defeat of [[Real Sociedad]] at Camp Nou.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://edition.cnn.com/2013/09/24/sport/football/barceona-sociedad-liga-football/|title=Lionel Messi, Neymar lead Barcelona past Real Sociedad|date=24 September 2013|access-date=27 October 2013|publisher=CNN|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130928082837/http://edition.cnn.com/2013/09/24/sport/football/barceona-sociedad-liga-football/|archive-date=28 September 2013}}</ref> On 26 October, in his first ''[[El Clásico]]'' appearance, he scored the opening goal and assisted the team's winning goal in a 2–1 win over Real Madrid.<ref name="clasico">{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/24603052|title=Barcelona 2–1 Real Madrid|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131026195023/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/0/football/24603052|archive-date=26 October 2013}}</ref> On 11 December, he scored a [[List of UEFA Champions League hat-tricks|hat-trick]] in a 6–1 win over [[Celtic F.C.|Celtic]] in the [[2013–14 UEFA Champions League group stage#Group H|group stage]].<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/0/football/25303700|title=Barcelona 6–1 Celtic|date=21 December 2013|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131211212109/http://www.bbc.com/sport/0/football/25303700|archive-date=11 December 2013}}</ref> A few days later, on 14 December, Neymar scored both of Barcelona's goals in their 2–1 win against [[Villarreal CF|Villarreal]].<ref>{{Cite news |date=13 December 2013 |title=Barcelona 2-1 Villarreal |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/25366236 |access-date=28 May 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB |archive-date=28 May 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260528134428/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/25366236 |url-status=live }}</ref>

15 games into the [[La Liga]] season, Neymar had produced a respectable four goals and eight assists, nailing down the left-wing position in the Barcelona team.<ref name=":41">{{Cite web|author1=Ryan Dabbs|date=17 January 2025|title='I spent the first six months at Barca with butterflies in my stomach. I was like… f*ck, I'm doing everything all wrong - I cried at half-time thinking, "What am I doing?"': Neymar reveals difficult start to life in Spain, with Lionel Messi helping him|url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/i-spent-the-first-six-months-at-barca-with-butterflies-in-my-stomach-i-was-like-f-ck-im-doing-everything-all-wrong-i-cried-at-half-time-thinking-what-am-i-doing-neymar-reveals-difficult-start-to-life-in-spain-with-lionel-messi-helping-him|access-date=13 June 2025|website=FourFourTwo|archive-date=17 July 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250717100904/https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/i-spent-the-first-six-months-at-barca-with-butterflies-in-my-stomach-i-was-like-f-ck-im-doing-everything-all-wrong-i-cried-at-half-time-thinking-what-am-i-doing-neymar-reveals-difficult-start-to-life-in-spain-with-lionel-messi-helping-him|url-status=live}}</ref> However, Neymar felt low, expressing in an interview over a decade later: "I spent the first 6 months at Barça with butterflies in my stomach".<ref name=":41" /> On 26 March 2014, Neymar scored twice in a 3–0 win against [[RC Celta de Vigo|Celta Vigo]].<ref>{{Cite news |date=25 March 2014 |title=Barcelona 3-0 Celta Vigo |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/26713925 |access-date=28 May 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB |archive-date=28 May 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260528134428/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/26713925 |url-status=live }}</ref> On 1 April, he scored in the Champions League quarter-final first leg against Atlético Madrid to level the match 1–1, though Atlético defeated Barcelona 1–0 in the second leg to eliminate them.<ref>{{Cite web |last=UEFA.com |title=History: Barcelona 1-1 Atleti: UEFA Champions League 2013/14 Quarter-finals |url=https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/match/2011871--barcelona-vs-atleti/ |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=UEFA |language=en |archive-date=28 May 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260528134428/https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/match/2011871--barcelona-vs-atleti/ |url-status=live }}</ref> On 16 April, Neymar played in the [[2014 Copa del Rey final]] against Real Madrid, which Barcelona lost.<ref>{{Cite news |date=16 April 2014 |title=Gareth Bale helps Real Madrid beat Barcelona in Copa del Rey |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/27053737 |access-date=28 May 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB |archive-date=28 May 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260528134428/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/27053737 |url-status=live }}</ref> He featured heavily in his debut season with Barcelona, ending the campaign with 15 goals in 41 appearances.<ref>{{Cite web|last=Jugadors|first=F. C. B.|title=Neymar Da Silva Santos Júnior stats {{!}} FC Barcelona Players|url=https://players.fcbarcelona.com/en/player/603-neymar-neymar-da-silva-santos-junior|access-date=13 June 2025|website=FCB Jugadors|archive-date=16 June 2025|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250616163905/https://players.fcbarcelona.com/en/player/603-neymar-neymar-da-silva-santos-junior|url-status=live}}</ref> At the end of the year, [[Eurosport]] ranked Neymar as the best under-23 player in the world, ahead of the likes of [[Paul Pogba]] and [[Mario Götze]].{{cn|date=November 2026}}

==== 2014–15: ''MSN'' trio and continental treble ====
The [[2014–15 FC Barcelona season|2014–15 season]] notably marked the birth of the new attacking trio of [[Lionel Messi]], new signing [[Luis Suárez (Uruguayan footballer)|Luis Suárez]] and Neymar — dubbed ''[[MSN (association football)|MSN]]''. A sports writer noted that Neymar's understanding with Messi, in comparison to the previous season, was "close to telepathic at times", and the addition of Suárez's "tenacity and hunger" resulted in "possibly the most formidable strike-force in European football".<ref name="UEFAErrors">{{Cite web |last=UEFA.com |title=The official website for European football |url=https://www.uefa.com/errors/ |access-date=13 May 2026 |publisher=UEFA |language=en |archive-date=29 July 2020 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200729205134/https://www.uefa.com/errors/ |url-status=live }}</ref>[[File:Neymar - FC Barcelona - 2015.jpg|thumb|272x272px|Neymar during a La Liga match against [[Villarreal CF|Villarreal]] in February 2015]]On 13 September, Neymar scored both of Barcelona's goals in their 2–0 win against [[Athletic Bilbao]] in [[2014–15 La Liga|La Liga]].<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/402599/athletic-club-barcelona#:~:text=Barca%202-0%20Athletic%20Bilbao,with%20a%20hard-fought%20win. |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN |title=Barcelona 2-0 Athletic (13 Sep, 2014) Final Score - ESPN (UK) |archive-date=28 May 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260528134428/https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/402599/athletic-club-barcelona#:~:text=Barca%202-0%20Athletic%20Bilbao,with%20a%20hard-fought%20win. |url-status=live }}</ref> Two weeks later, he scored a hat-trick against [[Granada CF|Granada]] in a 6–0 win on 27 September.<ref>{{cite news |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/29311918 |title=Barcelona 6–0 Granada |date=27 September 2014 |access-date=27 September 2014 |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140928084412/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/0/football/29311918 |archive-date=28 September 2014 }}</ref> On 24 January 2015, Neymar scored twice and assisted a goal in a 6–0 win at [[Elche CF|Elche]].<ref name="auto">{{Cite news|url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2015/jan/24/elche-barcelona-la-liga-match-report|title=Barcelona hit Elche for six with Lionel Messi and Neymar doubles|date=25 January 2015|work=The Guardian|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170305033212/https://www.theguardian.com/football/2015/jan/24/elche-barcelona-la-liga-match-report|archive-date=5 March 2017}}</ref> He continued to deliver for Barcelona, scoring twice in the [[2014–15 Copa del Rey|Copa del Rey]] round of 16, twice in the quarter-final and twice in the semi-final to reach the [[2015 Copa del Rey Final|final]].<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/0/football/31721178|title=Villarreal 1–3 Barcelona|date=4 March 2015|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150305172027/http://www.bbc.com/sport/0/football/31721178|archive-date=5 March 2015}}</ref><ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/football/european/11375834/Atletico-Madrid-2-Barcelona-3-match-report-Neymar-double-sees-of-nine-man-Atletico-in-Copa-del-Rey.html|title=Atletico Madrid 2 }}</ref> After defeating Atlético Madrid 1–0 to secure the league title on 17 May, Barcelona defeated Athletic Bilbao 3–1 at Camp Nou in the [[2015 Copa del Rey final]] on 30 May, with Neymar scoring the club's second goal to tie him as the competition's [[2014–15 Copa del Rey#Top goalscorers|top goalscorer]] with 7.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/0/football/32938036|title=Ath Bilbao 1–3 Barcelona|date=30 May 2015|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150602025437/http://www.bbc.com/sport/0/football/32938036|archive-date=2 June 2015}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |title=Copa del Rey 2014/2015 » Statistics: Topscorer |url=https://www.worldfootball.net/competition/co23/spain-copa-del-rey/se15370/2014-2015/statistics-goals/ |access-date=28 May 2026 |website=worldfootball.net |language=en |archive-date=28 May 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260528134428/https://www.worldfootball.net/competition/co23/spain-copa-del-rey/se15370/2014-2015/statistics-goals/ |url-status=live }}</ref>

Neymar was vital for Barcelona in the [[2014–15 UEFA Champions League|Champions League]], scoring six goals in the knock out stages against [[Paris Saint-Germain FC|Paris Saint-Germain]] (PSG) and [[FC Bayern Munich|Bayern Munich]] as Barcelona proceeded to the [[2015 UEFA Champions League Final|2015 Champions League final]] on 6 June.<ref>{{Cite web|date=21 April 2015|title=Barcelona 2-0 Paris St-Germain (agg 5-1)|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/32380834|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201027012557/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/32380834|archive-date=27 October 2020|access-date=14 January 2021|website=BBC}}</ref><ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/0/football/32683310|title=Bayern Mun 3–2 Barcelona|date=12 May 2015|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150512210015/http://www.bbc.com/sport/0/football/32683310|archive-date=12 May 2015}}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/0221-0e909116af7b-fe3ef95b6374-1000--messi-double-puts-barca-in-control-against-bayern/|title=Messi double puts Barça in control against Bayern|date=6 May 2015|publisher=UEFA|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150509001803/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/season%3D2015/matches/round%3D2000551/match%3D2014428/postmatch/report/index.html|archive-date=9 May 2015}}</ref> In the final, Neymar scored the third goal for Barcelona in the 3–1 defeat of Italian champions [[Juventus FC|Juventus]] at Berlin's [[Olympiastadion (Berlin)|Olympiastadion]], ensuring the club won its fifth Champions League title and become the first club in history to win the [[Treble (association football)|treble]] twice.<ref name="triple">{{cite news|url=http://www.fcbarcelona.com/football/first-team/detail/article/barca-make-history-with-second-treble|title=Barça make history with second treble!|date=6 June 2015|work=FC Barcelona|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150607120500/http://www.fcbarcelona.com/football/first-team/detail/article/barca-make-history-with-second-treble|archive-date=7 June 2015|access-date=7 June 2015}}</ref> Neymar became one of the few players to win both the [[Copa Libertadores]] and the Champions League,<ref>{{cite news|url=http://www.goal.com/en/news/1716/champions-league/2015/06/07/12492922/neymar-joins-exclusive-libertadores-champions-league-club|title=Neymar joins exclusive Libertadores-Champions League club|date=7 June 2015|work=Goal |url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150609201218/http://www.goal.com/en/news/1716/champions-league/2015/06/07/12492922/neymar-joins-exclusive-libertadores-champions-league-club|archive-date=9 June 2015}}</ref> and the first player to score in both quarter-final matches, both semi-final matches and the final of the Champions League.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Corrigan |first=Dermot |date=16 June 2015 |title=Lionel Messi will win 2015 Ballon d'Or, says Barcelona's Neymar |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37421272/lionel-messi-win-2015-ballon-dor-says-neymar |access-date=4 July 2026 |publisher=ESPN}}</ref>

Neymar ended the season with 39 goals in all competitions, including 10 in the Champions League, making him the joint top scorer with Ronaldo and teammate Messi in the latter competition.<ref>{{cite news|url=http://www.afp.com/en/news/hot-shot-neymar-realises-champions-league-dream|title=Hot-shot Neymar realises Champions League dream|date=6 June 2015|agency=Agence France-Presse|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150610180743/http://www.afp.com/en/news/hot-shot-neymar-realises-champions-league-dream|archive-date=10 June 2015}}</ref><ref name=":43">{{Cite web |author1=Andrew Murray |date=4 December 2015 |title=FourFourTwo's Best 100 Football Players in the World 2015 No.3: Neymar |url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/features/fourfourtwos-best-100-football-players-world-2015-no3-neymar |access-date=13 June 2025 |website=FourFourTwo |archive-date=7 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250707145718/https://www.fourfourtwo.com/features/fourfourtwos-best-100-football-players-world-2015-no3-neymar |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/0252-0cd2c6a4864d-8c8d00efd1bd-1000--barcelona-see-off-juventus-to-claim-fifth-title/|title=Barcelona see off Juventus to claim fifth attacking trio, ''MSN'', ended the season with a combined 122 goals, the most in a season for an attacking trio in Spanish football history.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2015/jun/06/barcelona-luis-suarez-lionel-messi-juventus-champions-league-final|title=Barcelona's Luis Suárez, Leo Messi and Neymar too good for Juventus|date=6 June 2015|work=The ==== 2015–17: Ballon d'Or candidate, domestic double and final season ====
In the [[2015–16 FC Barcelona season|2015–16 season]], on 12 September, Neymar scored a near-immediate equaliser against Atlético Madrid in La Liga before Barcelona went on to win the match 2–1.<ref>{{Cite news |date=11 September 2015 |title=Atletico Madrid 1-2 Barcelona |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/34220239 |access-date=28 May 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB}}</ref> In late September, Messi suffered a serious knee injury in the league, keeping him out for two months.<ref>{{Cite news |agency=Associated Press |date=26 September 2015 |title=Barcelona Loses Lionel Messi 2 Months With Injury |url=https://www.nytimes.com/2015/09/27/sports/soccer/lionel-messi-injury-barcelona.html |access-date=7 June 2025 |work=The New York Times |issn=0362-4331 |archive-date=14 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250714112144/https://www.nytimes.com/2015/09/27/sports/soccer/lionel-messi-injury-barcelona.html |url-status=live }}</ref> In Messi's absence, Neymar experienced one of the greatest peaks in his career, scoring 13 goals and providing 9 assists in just 8 games.<ref name=":19">{{Cite news |last=Lowe |first=Sid |date=20 November 2015 |title=Neymar transforms from Lionel Messi's butler to Barcelona's lord of the manor |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/blog/2015/nov/20/neymar-lionel-messi-barcelona-clasico-real-madrid |access-date=8 June 2025 |work=The Guardian |issn=0261-3077}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |last=Chabba |first=Gursher |date=11 September 2024 |title=13 goals in 8 games: Remembering Neymar's heroic run when Messi injured his knee in 2015 |url=https://tribuna.com/en/news/fcbarcelona-2024-09-11-13-goals-in-8-games-remembering-neymars-heroic-run-when-messi-injured-his-knee-in-2015/ |access-date=7 June 2025 |website=Tribuna.com}}</ref> His run included a four goal display in Barcelona's 5–2 home win in the league over [[Rayo Vallecano]];<ref>{{cite news|title=Barcelona 5–2 Rayo Vallecano|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/34549230|date=17 October 2015|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20151017064506/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/0/football/34549230|archive-date=17 October 2015}}</ref> a two goal display in a 3–0 win against [[FC BATE Borisov|BATE Borisov]] in the [[2015–16 UEFA Champions League|Champions League]] groupstage;<ref>{{Cite web |last=UEFA.com |title=History: Barcelona 3-0 BATE: UEFA Champions League 2015/16 Group stage |url=https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/match/2015721--barcelona-vs-bate/ |access-date=24 July 2026 |website=UEFA.com |language=en |archive-date=17 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250717194214/https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/match/2015721--barcelona-vs-bate/ |url-status=live }}</ref> two goals in a 3–0 win against Villarreal, including a "stunning" goal where he received the ball from just inside the area and flicked it up over his shoulder and over a defender before volleying it into the goal;<ref>{{Cite web |date=8 November 2015 |title=Neymar scores stunning volley for Barcelona against Villarreal |url=https://www.skysports.com/football/news/11833/10061405/watch-sublime-neymar-volley |website=[[Sky Sports]] |access-date=4 June 2025 |archive-date=7 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250707143434/https://www.skysports.com/football/news/11833/10061405/watch-sublime-neymar-volley |url-status=live }}</ref> two goals in a 4–0 victory against Real Sociedad; and a goal alongside an assist in Barcelona's 4–0 away win against Real Madrid.<ref>{{cite news|title=Suarez scores twice as Barca thrash Real|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/34849342|agency=BBC|access-date=21 November 2015|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20151120223751/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/0/football/34849342|archive-date=20 November 2015}}</ref> Villarreal manager [[Marcelino García Toral|Marcelino]] lauded Neymar as "probably the most in-form attacker in the league, decisive in front of goal and also in their [buildup] play".<ref name=":19"/> Neymar was named the [[La Liga Player of the Month]] for November, which made him the first Barcelona player to receive the award since it was inaugurated in September 2013.<ref>{{Cite news |date=2015-12-08 |title=Neymar is first Barcelona player to win La Liga player of month award |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/35040246 |access-date=2026-09-15 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260915161214/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/35040246 |archive-date=15 September 2026 |url-status=live}}</ref> In December, he featured in the [[2015 FIFA Club World Cup]], delivering two assists in Barcelona's 3–0 win against [[Club Atlético River Plate|River Plate]] in the [[2015 FIFA Club World Cup final|final]] on 20 December.<ref>{{Cite web |last1=Español |first1=En |last2=Español |first2=En |last3=Shared |last4=Shared |date=20 December 2015 |title=Neymar: "We're making history" |url=https://e01-marca.uecdn.es/en/football/barcelona/2015/12/20/5676db4f46163f2d3b8b457a.html |access-date=28 May 2026 |website=MARCA English |language=en}}</ref> [[File:Seleção olímpica masculina de futebol faz primeiro treino em Brasília (28674892006) (cropped).jpg|thumb|300x300px|Neymar training with the [[Brazil national under-23 football team|Brazil under-23]] in August 2016, soon after his contract extension with Barcelona]]
At 23 years old, Neymar was being praised for elevating to the same level of Messi and Ronaldo, possibly entering the best player in the world debate.<ref name=":15">{{Cite news |date=19 November 2015 |title=Neymar, Messi, Ronaldo: Is Brazilian now world's best player? |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/34835193 |access-date=31 May 2025 |work=BBC Sport |archive-date=31 May 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250531102933/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/34835193 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |last=Bull |first=Jj |date=19 November 2015 |title=How Neymar usurped Cristiano Ronaldo as the (second) best player in the world |url=https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/football/players/neymar/12002663/How-Neymar-usurped-Cristiano-Ronaldo-as-the-second-best-player-in-the-world.html |access-date=25 July 2026 |website=[[The Daily Telegraph|The Telegraph]] |archive-date=15 August 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250815150321/https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/football/players/neymar/12002663/How-Neymar-usurped-Cristiano-Ronaldo-as-the-second-best-player-in-the-world.html |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=31 December 2015 |title=theScore's Top 20 footballers of 2015: No. 5-1 {{!}} theScore |url=https://www.thescore.com/fran/news/924378/the-scores-top-20-footballers-of-2015-no-5-1 |access-date=5 August 2026 |website=www.thescore.com |language=en}}</ref> He was shortlisted for the [[2015 FIFA Ballon d'Or]] alongside Messi and Ronaldo. Several journalists argued that he deserved to win the award, particularly for his crucial performances in the knockout stages of the 2014–15 Champions League and during Messi's absence during the first half of 2015–16.<ref>{{Cite web |date=11 January 2016 |title=Why Lionel Messi, Neymar and Cristiano Ronaldo each deserve to win the 2015 Ballon d'Or |url=https://foxsports.com.au/football/why-lionel-messi-neymar-and-cristiano-ronaldo-each-deserve-to-win-the-2015-ballon-dor/news-story/9731994057d07b8d80b96aae475d925b |access-date=3 July 2025 |website=Fox Sports |archive-date=3 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250703101917/https://www.foxsports.com.au/football/why-lionel-messi-neymar-and-cristiano-ronaldo-each-deserve-to-win-the-2015-ballon-dor/news-story/9731994057d07b8d80b96aae475d925b |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |last=Laurence |first=Martin |date=11 January 2016 |title=Does Neymar deserve to win the Ballon d'Or ahead of Lionel Messi and Cristiano Ronaldo? |url=https://www.skysports.com/football/news/11096/10121522/does-neymar-deserve-to-win-the-ballon-dor-ahead-of-lionel-messi-and-cristiano-ronaldo |website=[[Sky Sports]] |access-date=3 July 2025 |archive-date=3 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250703101917/https://www.skysports.com/football/news/11096/10121522/does-neymar-deserve-to-win-the-ballon-dor-ahead-of-lionel-messi-and-cristiano-ronaldo |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |last=Ahmed |first=Shahan |date=12 November 2015 |title=Neymar continues to make a case for 2015 Ballon d'Or over injured Messi |url=http://sports.yahoo.com/blogs/soccer-fc-yahoo/neymar-makes-case-for-2015-ballon-d-or-191013576.html |access-date=3 July 2025 |website=Yahoo Sports |archive-date=3 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250703101917/https://sports.yahoo.com/blogs/soccer-fc-yahoo/neymar-makes-case-for-2015-ballon-d-or-191013576.html |url-status=live }}</ref> He came in third place, receiving 7.86 percent of the vote.<ref name="FIFA 2015">[https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/35285469 "Lionel Messi wins Ballon d'Or over Cristiano Ronaldo & Neymar"] {{Webarchive|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230529201243/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/35285469 |date=29 May 2023 }} . BBC Sport. Retrieved 12 January 2016</ref> Both ''[[L'Équipe]]'' and ''[[Sky Sports]]'' ranked Neymar as the second-best player in the world of 2015, only behind Messi.<ref name=":53">{{Cite web |date=26 December 2015 |title=Messi, Neymar Named Top Players Of Year |url=https://news.sky.com/story/messi-neymar-named-top-players-of-year-10334797 |website=Sky Sports}}</ref><ref name=":60">{{Cite web |title=Leo Messi, Neymar Jr and Luis Suárez are top 3 players, according to l'Equipe |url=https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/news/1072964/leo-messi-neymar-jr-and-luis-suarez-are-top-players-according-to-lequipe |access-date=26 July 2026 |website=www.fcbarcelona.com |language=en |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260726220213/https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/news/1072964/leo-messi-neymar-jr-and-luis-suarez-are-top-players-according-to-lequipe |archive-date=26 July 2026 |url-status=live}}</ref>

In the quarter-final of the [[2015–16 Copa del Rey|Copa del Rey]] in January 2016, Neymar scored twice in Barcelona's 5–2 aggregate win over Athletic Bilbao.<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/441180/athletic-club-barcelona |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN|title=Barcelona 3-1 Athletic (27 Jan, 2016) Final Score - ESPN (UK)}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/441186/barcelona-athletic-club |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN|title=Athletic 1-2 Barcelona (20 Jan, 2016) Final Score - ESPN (UK)}}</ref> On 12 March, in a 6–0 win, he scored twice against [[Getafe CF|Getafe]] in the league.<ref>{{Cite web |title=FC Barcelona - Getafe {{!}} La Liga Matchday 29 - FC Barcelona |url=https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/matches/35291/fc-barcelona-getafe-la-liga-2015-2016 |access-date=24 July 2026 |website=www.fcbarcelona.com |archive-date=24 July 2026 Liga campaign, being directly involved in 40 goals (24 goals and 16 assists) as Barcelona won the title, finishing just one point ahead of Real Madrid.<ref name=":69">{{Cite web |title=WhoScored's |language=en |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260805145226/https://www.whoscored.com/articles/H7-ZhibGVU2OQUEZxbpWsw/show/whoscoreds-european-team-of-the-season |archive-date=5 August 2026 |url-status=live}}</ref> He also won eight penalties for Barcelona, more than any other player in the league.<ref name=":69" /> He was included in ''[[The Guardian]]''<nowiki/>'s La Liga Team of the Season.<ref>{{Cite news |last=Laurence |first=Martin |date=18 May 2016 |title=Teams of the season: best XIs from the Premier League and around Europe |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/who-scored-blog/2016/may/18/premier-league-team-season-2015-16 |access-date=5 July 2026 |work=The Guardian |language=en-GB |issn=0261-3077}}</ref> On 22 May, he scored in Barcelona's 2–0 win against [[Sevilla FC|Sevilla]] in the [[2016 Copa del Rey final]], celebrating a [[Double (association football)#Spain|domestic double]] triumph.<ref>{{cite web |url=http://www.marca.com/eventos/marcador/futbol/2015_16/copa/final/bar_sev/|title=Messi sirve el doblete |trans-title=Messi hands out double|work=Marca|location=Spain|language=es|date=22 May The front three of Messi, Suárez and Neymar finished the season with 131 goals, breaking the record they had set the previous year for most goals by an attacking trio in a single season.<ref>{{cite news|title=Messi, Suárez and Neymar Jr end season with 131 goals|work=Barca Fan dip in form at the end of the season, Neymar ultimately reached over 30 goals for the second consecutive season, scoring 31, as well as 27 assists.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Atchinson |first=Jack |date=16 May 2016 |title=Lionel Messi, Luis Suarez and Neymar: Barcelona trio's best goals }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|last=Paul|first=Sumeet|date=19 April 2016|title=Barcelona legend defends under-fire Neymar as criticism gathers
