the second consecutive year, ''Sky Sports'' ranked him at number two in their 2016 Top 100 Players of the Year list.<ref>{{Cite web |date=24 December 2016 |title=Top 100 players of 2016 |url=https://www.skysports.com/football/news/11096/10704085/top-100-players-of-2016-full-list-as-ranked-by-whoscored-com |website=Sky Sports |access-date=2 July 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260703032148/https://www.skysports.com/football/news/11096/10704085/top-100-players-of-2016-full-list-as-ranked-by-whoscored-com |archive-date=3 July 2026 |url-status=live}}</ref> 

On 1 July 2016, Neymar signed a new contract with Barcelona until 2021. His new contract included a €200&nbsp;million buy-out clause, rising to €222m in the second year of his deal, and €250m thereafter. His future at Barcelona had been in doubt over rumours that the likes of PSG, Real Madrid and [[Manchester City F.C.|Manchester City]] were considering to make huge offers for him.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Neymar extends contract until 2021 |url=https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/news/1064652/neymar-extends-contract-until-2021 |access-date=23 February 2026 |website=FC Barcelona |language=en |archive-date=11 October 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20251011013330/https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/news/1064652/neymar-extends-contract-until-2021 |url-status=live }}</ref>

On 12 September of the [[2016–17 Paris Saint-Germain FC season|2016–17 season]], Neymar registered a goal and four assists in Barcelona's 7–0 win against Celtic in the group stage of the [[2016–17 UEFA Champions League|Champions League]].<ref>{{Cite news |date=12 September 2016 |title=Champions League: Barcelona 7-0 Celtic |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/37331224 |access-date=9 June 2025 |work=BBC Sport |archive-date=28 June 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260628210746/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/37331224 |url-status=live }}</ref> In an away fixture in La Liga against [[Sporting de Gijón|Sporting Gijón]] on 24 September, he scored twice in a 5–0 win.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Sporting de Gijón - FC Barcelona {{!}} La Liga Matchday 4 - FC Barcelona |url=https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/matches/35443/sporting-de-gijon-fc-barcelona-la-liga-2016-2017 |access-date=24 July 2026 |website=www.fcbarcelona.com |archive-date=24 July 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260724134341/https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/matches/35443/sporting-de-gijon-fc-barcelona-la-liga-2016-2017 |url-status=live }}</ref> On 19 January 2017, he scored his club's only goal in a 1–0 win in the first leg of the [[2016–17 Copa del Rey|Copa del Rey]] quarter-final against Real Sociedad, converting from the penalty spot.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Real Sociedad - FC Barcelona {{!}} Copa Del Rey Quarter-finals - FC Barcelona |url=https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/matches/36261/real-sociedad-fc-barcelona-cdr-2016-2017 |access-date=24 July 2026 |website=www.fcbarcelona.com}}</ref> Neymar's form drastically dipped, having scored just five goals in La Liga by February.<ref name=":49">{{Cite web |title=While Messi and Suarez continue to deliver, Neymar must buck up ideas |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37466864/while-messi-suarez-continue-deliver-neymar-buck-ideas |access-date=24 July 2026 |website=www.espn.co.uk}}</ref> [[ESPN]] said that Neymar looked "rusty in front of goal".<ref name=":49" /> While tiredness was cited as an explanation, sections of the media instead attributed his decline to the aggressive treatment he received from oppositions, with ''[[Mundo Deportivo]]'' describing him as "the most hunted player in La Liga".<ref name=":49" /> [[File:Barça 6 - PSG 1; Dimecres 8 de març de 2017 - 33340360535 (cropped).jpg|thumb|250x250px|Neymar celebrating after Barcelona's sixth goal against [[Paris Saint-Germain FC|PSG]] in the [[UEFA Champions League]] second leg win in March 2017|left]]
Neymar was pivotal in Barcelona's [[Barcelona 6–1 Paris Saint-Germain|6–1 victory]] (widely known as ''La Remontada'' English translation: ''The Comeback'') over PSG in the Champions League round of 16 on 8 March, overcoming the 4–0 loss in the first leg.<ref>{{Cite web |date=8 March 2017 |title=Barcelona vs. Paris Saint-Germain – Football Match Report – March 8, 2017 |url=http://www.espn.co.uk/football/report?gameId=469090 |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201229134950/https://www.espn.co.uk/football/report?gameId=469090 |archive-date=29 December 2020 |access-date=5 June 2018 |publisher=ESPN}}</ref> It is widely regarded as the greatest comeback in football history.<ref>{{Cite web |date=28 May 2026 |title=11 Greatest Comebacks In Football History (Ranked) |url=https://onefootball.com/es/noticias/11-greatest-comebacks-in-football-history-ranked-37700305 |access-date=19 July 2026 |website=OneFootball |language=es |archive-date=19 July 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260719221218/https://onefootball.com/es/noticias/11-greatest-comebacks-in-football-history-ranked-37700305 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=9 April 2025 |title=Biggest Comebacks in Champions League History |url=https://www.si.com/soccer/biggest-comebacks-champions-league-history |access-date=19 July 2026 |website=SI |language=en-US |archive-date=19 July 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260719194739/https://www.si.com/soccer/biggest-comebacks-champions-league-history |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=8 April 2025 |title=Remembering la remontada: Barcelona 6-1 PSG |url=https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/football/european/barcelona-psg-2017-champions-league-comeback-b2729254.html |access-date=19 July 2026 |website=The Independent |language=en |archive-date=19 July 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260719211935/https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/football/european/barcelona-psg-2017-champions-league-comeback-b2729254.html |url-status=live }}</ref> Barcelona were 5–3 down on aggregate in the 88th minute, but scored three goals in the final seven minutes to progress into the quarter-final — Neymar contributed to all three goals, scoring twice and providing an assist in the 95th minute.<ref name=":37">{{Cite web |last=Gwilliam |first=Louise |date=9 March 2017 |title=Barcelona 6-1 Paris St-Germain (6-5 agg) |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/39152666 |website=[[BBC Sport]] |access-date=9 June 2025 |archive-date=9 March 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210309115421/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/39152666 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{cite web |last=Ryan |first=Danny |date=30 March 2017 |title=Neymar's seven defining minutes of unparalleled genius against Paris Saint-Germain |url=https://thesefootballtimes.co/neymars-seven-defining-minutes-of-unparalleled-genius-against-paris-saint-germain/ |url-status=live [[Messi–Ronaldo rivalry|Messi and Ronaldo]].<ref name=":14">{{Cite web |date=17 February 2020 |title=Neymar must begin to write his PSG Champions League legacy now |url=https://www.goal.com/en-gb/news/neymar-must-begin-to-write-his-psg-champions-league-legacy-now/jl9shpvj2b1l13kvuh1hpsv9n |access-date=2 June 2025 |website=Goal |archive-date=7 July 2025 }}</ref> PSG's midfielder [[Marco Verratti]] later said, "If I could sign one player for PSG it would be Neymar [...] He is at the same level as Messi."<ref name=":66">{{Cite web |author1=FourFourTwo Staff |date=11 April 2017 |title=Verratti wants Neymar at Paris Saint-Germain |url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/verratti-wants-neymar-paris-saint-germain |access-date=27 April 2026 |website=FourFourTwo |language=en |archive-date=23 January 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260123132331/https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/verratti-wants-neymar-paris-saint-germain On 15 May, Neymar scored his first away hat-trick for Barcelona in a 4–1 victory against [[UD Las Palmas|Las Palmas]], ensuring that Barcelona were still in the La Liga title race, but Real Madrid ultimately won the title on 20 May.<ref>{{Cite news |date=20 May 2017 |title=Malaga 0-2 Real Madrid |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/39926301 |access-date=7 June 2025 |work=BBC Sport |archive-date=26 December 2018 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181226192537/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/39926301 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref name=":29">{{Cite web |last=been "great" |first=Neymar's hat-trick against Las Palmas continued a stunning 2016-17 season for the forward, who feels his campaign has |title=Neymar: This is my best Barcelona season scored his 105th, and final, goal for Barcelona in the [[2017 Copa del Rey Final|2017 Copa del Rey final]], as Barcelona defeated [[Deportivo Alavés|Alavés]] 3–1.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/39909498|title=Barcelona 3 Alaves 1|date=27 May 2017|work=BBC Sport|access-date=31 May 2017|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170530212245/http://www.bbc.com/sport/football/39909498|archive-date=30 May 2017}}</ref> This made him the first player to score in three consecutive Copa del Rey finals since [[Ferenc Puskás]] in the 1960s.<ref>{{Cite web |date=28 May 2017 |title=Neymar, Messi equal Copa del Rey records being less prolific than his previous two seasons, prominent journalist [[Guillem Balagué]] credited Neymar for being more of a provider and for maturing his game.<ref name=":61">{{Cite web |date=1 2026 |url-status=live}}</ref> In Balagué's view, Neymar was "now second only to Messi as the best player in the world."<ref name=":61" /> According to [[Opta Sports#Association football|''WhoScored'']], which is powered by [[Opta Sports]] data, Neymar was Europe's best-performing player during the season.<ref name=":42">{{Cite web |title=European Team of the Season: FWL - Neymar (Barcelona) |url=https://www.whoscored.com/articles/hc0lXmGum0aWPwbkjUnp7A/show/european-team-of-the-season-fwl-neymar-(barcelona) |access-date=4 August 2026 |website=www.whoscored.com August 2026 |url-status=live}}</ref> 

By July 2017, media speculation emerged that Neymar wanted to leave Barcelona for PSG, who he had been "close to joining" the previous year according to his agent [[Wagner Ribeiro (football agent)|Wagner Ribeiro]].<ref>{{Cite web |title=Neymar to PSG? Barcelona's denials don't stop transfer talk |url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/soccer/neymar-psg-barcelonas-denials-dont-stop-transfer-talk/ |access-date=11 June 2026 |language=en |archive-date=30 November 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221130214604/https://www.sportsnet.ca/soccer/neymar-psg-barcelonas-denials-dont-stop-transfer-talk/ |url-status=live }}</ref> Neymar was driven to join PSG as he wanted a new challenge and to play with several of his Brazil national team teammates who were at the club, including new signing [[Dani Alves]].<ref>{{Cite web |author1=FourFourTwo Staff |date=6 August 2017 |title=Alves: Neymar told me to join PSG before he left Barca |url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/alves-neymar-told-me-join-psg-he-left-barca |access-date=2 June 2026 }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=3 August 2017 |title=Neymar on move: 'I need new challenges' |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37528582/neymar-barcelona-always-my-heart-need-new-challenges |access-date=6 July 2025 |publisher=ESPN |language=en |archive-date=6 July 2025 Neymar asked Barcelona to allow him to leave.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Dawson |first=Alan |title=Neymar has asked to leave FC Barcelona |url=https://www.businessinsider.com/psg-transfer-news-neymar-tells-fc-barcelona-that-he-is-leaving-2017-8 |access-date=12 November 2023 |website=Business Insider |archive-date=12 November 2023 president, Bartomeu, admitted he was powerless to stop him from wanting to leave.<ref>{{Cite news |last=Aarons |first=Ed |date=28 July 2017 |title=Neymar storms out of Barcelona training after clashing with team-mate |work=The Guardian |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2017/jul/28/neymar-barcelona-training-paris-saint-germain |access-date=12 November 2023 |issn=0261-3077 |archive-date=12 November 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231112142236/https://www.theguardian.com/football/2017/jul/28/neymar-barcelona-training-paris-saint-germain |url-status=live}}</ref> In the short time that Neymar was at Barcelona, he was a "crucial piece" to the club's success in those years, scoring 105 goals and assisting 76 in 186 appearances.<ref>{{Cite web |author1=FourFourTwo Staff |date=2 August 2017 |title=105 goals, 41 MSN assists - Opta breakdown of Neymar's Barcelona career |url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/105-goals-41-msn-assists-opta-breakdown-neymars-barcelona-career |access-date=31 May 2025 |website=FourFourTwo |archive-date=31 May 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250531103650/https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/105-goals-41-msn-assists-opta-breakdown-neymars-barcelona-career |url-status=live }}</ref><ref name=":8">{{Cite web |date=15 August 2023 |title=Neymar threw away his chance to become a Barcelona great! Saudi Arabia transfer the only option after world-record failure at PSG |url=https://www.goal.com/en/lists/neymar-threw-away-barcelona-great-saudi-arabia-transfer-psg-failure/blt4b2702bdafc3f4b3 |access-date=20 November 2023 |website=Goal |archive-date=20 November 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231120171638/https://www.goal.com/en/lists/neymar-threw-away-barcelona-great-saudi-arabia-transfer-psg-failure/blt4b2702bdafc3f4b3 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |last=Konstantelos |first=Nektarios |date=5 August 2017 |title=Neymar Jr: What Is His Legacy At Barcelona? |url=https://foxsports1340am.com/neymar-jr-legacy-barcelona/ |access-date=3 June 2025 }}</ref> ''[[Sports Illustrated]]'' said that he arguably eclipsed Ronaldo as the second-best player in the world during his final three seasons at Barcelona.<ref>{{Cite web |date=18 October 2024 |title=The 50 Greatest Soccer Players of All Time |url=https://www.si.com/soccer/the-50-greatest-soccer-players-of-all-time |access-date=1 August 2025 |website=SI |language=en-US |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250211203355/https://www.si.com/soccer/the-50-greatest-soccer-players-of-all-time |archive-date=11 February 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref>

===Paris Saint-Germain===
[[File:Neymar Jr Presentation (cropped).jpg|thumb|246x246px|Neymar posing with his PSG number 10 jersey alongside club president [[Nasser Al-Khelaifi]]]]
On 3 August 2017, Barcelona announced that Neymar's legal representatives made a payment of €222 million to the club, equal to the [[release clause]] of his contract, which constituted the [[List of most expensive association football transfers|most expensive transfer ever]].<ref>{{Cite web|date=20 March 2019|title=10 Most Valuable Football Transfer Contracts So Far|url=https://sportycious.com/most-valuable-football-transfer-958846|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200929115636/https://sportycious.com/most-valuable-football-transfer-958846|archive-date=29 September 2020|access-date=16 October 2019|website=Sportycious}}</ref><ref name="BBCTransfer2017">{{Cite news |date=3 August 2017 |title=Neymar: Paris St-Germain sign Barcelona forward for world record 222m euros |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/40762417 |access-date=10 June 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB |archive-date=9 October 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211009152244/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/40762417 |url-status=live }}</ref> The situation was unusual, in that the fee was paid to the club directly, after La Liga had refused to receive the payment — citing violation of [[UEFA Financial Fair Play Regulations|Financial Fair Play]] (FFP) rules by PSG.<ref>{{cite news|date=3 August 2017|title=Neymar: Barcelona say Paris St-Germain target has paid his buyout clause|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/40814854|url-status=live|access-date=25 July 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201125171706/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/40814854|archive-date=25 November 2020}}</ref><ref>{{cite news |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/40823628|title=Neymar: Paris St-Germain's new signing said he left Barcelona for a new challenge|date=4 August 2017|access-date=4 August 2017|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170805102048/http://www.bbc.com/sport/football/40823628|archive-date=5 August 2017}}</ref>

Neymar's unexpected departure from Barcelona and the record-breaking transfer fee made global headlines, with some in the media labelling it as "the signing of the century".<ref>{{Cite web |date=3 August 2017 |title=Neymar the superstar to take PSG to Champions League glory {{!}} Goal.com UK |url=https://www.goal.com/en-gb/news/neymar-psgneymar-the-superstar-to-bring-psg-champions-league-glory/n3s219wanflz1ri33f63i9na9 |access-date=1 June 2026 |website=Goal |language=en-GB |archive-date=7 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250707145623/https://www.goal.com/en-gb/news/neymar-psgneymar-the-superstar-to-bring-psg-champions-league-glory/n3s219wanflz1ri33f63i9na9 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref name="auto1">{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37479562/psg-neymar-join-forces-mutual-benefit-status-money-hopefully-lead-ucl-glory |access-date=1 June 2026 |publisher=ESPN|title=PSG hope Neymar brings UCL glory in exchange for status, comfort, money|date=6 August 2017|last=Kuper|first=Simon}}</ref> He joined PSG on a contract that would run until 2022, earning roughly €36&nbsp;million a year — one of the [[List of largest sports contracts|largest sports contracts]] — to make him the second-highest paid player in the world.<ref>{{Cite news |last=Sport |first=Telegraph |date=24 September 2017 |title=PSG make Neymar world's second-highest paid player according to leaked document |url=https://www.telegraph.co.uk/football/2017/09/24/psg-make-neymar-worlds-second-highest-paid-player-according/ |access-date=9 July 2025 |work=The Telegraph |issn=0307-1235}}</ref><ref>{{cite news|url=https://en.psg.fr/pro/article/neymar-jr-signs-with-paris-saint-germain|title=Neymar Jr signs with Paris Saint-Germain!|date=3 August 2017|access-date=3 August 2017|publisher=Paris Saint-Germain|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170803205243/https://en.psg.fr/pro/article/neymar-jr-signs-with-paris-saint-germain|archive-date=3 August 2017}}</ref> This was a €12&nbsp;million increase from his salary at Barcelona.<ref>{{Cite web |date=25 June 2019 |title=Neymar accepts pay-cut in 'verbal agreement' with Barca - reports |url=https://www.france24.com/en/20190625-neymar-accepts-pay-cut-verbal-agreement-with-barca-reports |access-date=5 August 2026 |website=France 24 |language=en |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250824190343/https://www.france24.com/en/20190625-neymar-accepts-pay-cut-verbal-agreement-with-barca-reports |archive-date=24 August 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> 

As the club's main player, Neymar was touted to lead PSG to their first Champions League title.<ref name="auto1" /><ref>{{Cite news |last=Laurens |first=Julien |date=3 August 2017 |title=PSG convinced The relationship between Neymar and Barcelona fans was tainted following his move to PSG.<ref name=":8"/> In his presentation on 17 August, he said that leaving Barcelona was one of the toughest decisions in his life.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Masters |first=James |date=4 August 2017 |title=Neymar: Leaving Barcelona 'one of the toughest' decisions of my life |url=https://www.cnn.com/2017/08/04/football/neymar-paris-saint-germain/index.html |access-date=12 November 2023 |publisher=CNN |archive-date=9 February 2024 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240209183230/https://www.cnn.com/2017/08/04/football/neymar-paris-saint-germain/index.html |url-status=live }}</ref> He joined a growing list of Brazilian players who had signed for [[Paris Saint-Germain FC|PSG]] in previous years, including [[Thiago Silva]], [[Lucas Moura]] and [[David Luiz]].<ref>{{Cite web |last=move |first=Another chapter may be written in Paris Saint-Germain's lifelong love affair with Brazilian stars after Neymar's record-breaking |title=The boys from Brazil: Neymar joins PSG's roll |url=https://www.beinsports.com/en-mena/football/ligue-1/articles/the-boys-from-brazil-neymar-joins-psgs-roll-1 |access-date=25 July 2026 |website=beIN SPORTS |language=en-mena |archive-date=25 July 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260725114007/https://www.beinsports.com/en-mena/football/ligue-1/articles/the-boys-from-brazil-neymar-joins-psgs-roll-1 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite news |date=5 March 2018 |title=Paris St-Germain v Real Madrid: The curious love affair between PSG and Brazilian footballers |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/43217957 |access-date=25 July 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB}}</ref> 

====Contract breach lawsuit====
On 27 August 2017, Barcelona filed a lawsuit against Neymar, demanding he return the contract-renewal bonus he'd received as well as €8.5&nbsp;million in [[damages]] and an additional 10% in [[arrears]]. The club claimed the money is owed on the basis of the renewal of the contract they signed in 2016. The club also requested PSG to take on the financial responsibility for the payment of the fees if the player is shown unable to do so.<ref>{{Cite web|title=FC Barcelona statement {{!}} FC Barcelona|url=https://www.fcbarcelona.com/club/news/2017-2018/fc-barcelona-statement-22-08-2017|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201111225007/https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/news/748300/fc-barcelona-statement|archive-date=11 November 2020|access-date=23 August 2017|website=FC Barcelona}}</ref> Neymar's lawyers announced that they would contest the case,<ref>{{Cite news|date=23 August 2017|title=PSG's Neymar to contest Barcelona lawsuit over world-record move |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/41014149|url-status=live|access-date=19 May 2026|work=[[BBC News]]|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201206194758/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/41014149|archive-date=6 December 2020}}</ref> but on 19 June 2020, a Spanish court ruled that Neymar must return $7.5&nbsp;million (€6.7&nbsp;million) to Barcelona.<ref>{{cite news |first1=Sam|last1= Marsden|first2=Moises|last2=Llorens|date=19 June 2020 |title=Neymar ordered to pay Barcelona €6.7m following bonus dispute|url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37584337/neymar-ordered-pay-barcelona-67m-following-bonus-dispute |publisher=[[ESPN]]|location=London |access-date=19 May 2026}}</ref><ref>{{cite magazine |agency=[[Associated Press]]|date=19 June 2020 |title=Neymar Loses Lawsuit vs. Barcelona, Must Pay Club $7.5 Million in Signing Bonus Dispute|url=https://www.si.com/soccer/2020/06/19/neymar-lawsuit-ruling-barcelona-signing-bonus |magazine=[[Sports Illustrated]] |access-date=19 May 2026}}</ref> In 2021, the club and the player announced they've reached an "amicable" [[out-of-court settlement]], whose details were not publicly divulged.<ref>{{cite news|first1=Sam|last1=Marsden|first2=Moises|last2=Llorens|date=26 July 2021|title=Barcelona and Neymar reach 'amicable' out of court settlement|url=https://www.espn.in/football/story/_/id/37619399/barcelona-neymar-reach-amicable-court-settlement|publisher=[[ESPN]]|location=India|access-date=19 May 2026|archive-date=7 January 2026|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260107003651/https://www.espn.in/football/story/_/id/37619399/barcelona-neymar-reach-amicable-court-settlement|url-status=live}}</ref>

==== 2017–19: PSG's talisman, domestic success and injuries ====
[[File:Toulouse IMG 0340 (25393764237) (cropped).jpg|thumb|Neymar ahead of a [[Ligue 1]] fixture against [[Toulouse FC|Toulouse]] in February 2018|left]]
Neymar made his debut for PSG on 13 August 2017, scoring a goal and assisting another in a 3–0 away victory over [[En Avant de Guingamp|Guingamp]] in the league.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/40924357|title=Neymar feels 'more alive than ever' after scoring on PSG debut|date=14 August 2017|access-date=15 August 2017|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170816015714/http://www.bbc.com/sport/football/40924357|archive-date=16 August 2017}}</ref> Guingamp's defender [[Lucas Deaux]] said "I have never seen a player like this before".<ref>{{Cite web|last=UEFA.com|date=5 February 2023|title=Neymar goals, stats, records, claims to fame: How brilliant is the Paris star? {{!}} UEFA Champions League 2022/23|url=https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/024e-0e99612f5f48-479aef8e2056-1000--neymar-goals-stats-records-claims-to-fame-how-brilliant-is/|access-date=13 June 2025|website=UEFA.com|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250713201600/https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/024e-0e99612f5f48-479aef8e2056-1000--neymar-goals-stats-records-claims-to-fame-how-brilliant-is/|archive-date=13 July 2025|url-status=live}}</ref> In the next fixture on 20 August against [[Toulouse FC|Toulouse]], Neymar contributed with two goals and two assists to help PSG win 6–2.<ref>{{cite news|date=20 August 2017|title=Neymar at the double as PSG hit back to thump Toulouse|publisher=[[Eurosport]]|url=http://www.eurosport.com/football/neymar-at-the-double-as-psg-hit-back-to-thump-toulouse_sto6295474/story.shtml|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170822134634/http://www.eurosport.com/football/neymar-at-the-double-as-psg-hit-back-to-thump-toulouse_sto6295474/story.shtml|url-status=dead|archive-date=22 August 2017|access-date=21 August 2017}}</ref> On 30 September, Neymar scored twice in another 6–2 win as PSG defeated [[FC Girondins de Bordeaux|Bordeaux]].<ref>{{Cite web |last=Bordeaux |first=Thomas Meunier is wary of PSG forgetting their difficulties against Montpellier after a stunning 6-2 thrashing of |title=Neymar makes PSG confident, but not unplayable |url=https://www.beinsports.com/en-us/soccer/ligue-1/articles/neymar-makes-psg-confident-but-not-unplayab-2 |access-date=2 August 2026 |website=beIN SPORTS |language=en-us}}</ref> On 22 October, he scored against rivals [[Olympique de Marseille|Marseille]] in his first ''[[Le Classique]]'' in a 2–2 result, but was sent off — the first time in his PSG career.<ref>{{Cite web |date=15 April 2018 |title=PSG 2017-18: Neymar's highs and lows in a title-winning season |url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/psg-2017-18-neymars-highs-and-lows-a-title-winning-season |access-date=5 July 2025 |website=FourFourTwo |language=en}}</ref> On 16 December, Neymar scored twice and assisted twice to help PSG defeat [[Stade Rennais FC|Rennes]] 4–1.<ref>{{Cite web |author1=FourFourTwo Staff |date=16 December 2017 |title=Rennes 1 Paris Saint-Germain 4: Two goals, two assists for Neymar |url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/rennes-1-paris-saint-germain-4-two-goals-two-assists-neymar |access-date=8 July 2026 |website=FourFourTwo |language=en |archive-date=21 April 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260421163859/https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/rennes-1-paris-saint-germain-4-two-goals-two-assists-neymar |url-status=live }}</ref> As the talisman at PSG, Neymar had immediately established himself as the club's most influential player, enjoying more freedom than he had at Barcelona.

Neymar's [[2017–18 Paris Saint-Germain FC season|2017–18 season]] ended in late February 2018 after suffering a [[fifth metatarsal bone|metatarsal]] injury in his right foot in a league fixture against Marseille, causing him to miss PSG's crucial second leg tie against Real Madrid in the Champions League, who ultimately eliminated them.<ref>{{Cite news |title=Neymar stretchered off for PSG |access-date=4 June 2026 |work=Sky Sports |language=en}}</ref> He scored 28 goals in 30 matches, helping his new club win the [[domestic treble]] of the Ligue 1, Coupe de France, and [[Coupe de la Neymar was included in the [[European Sports Magazines#ESM Team of the Year|ESM Team of the Year]] for the first time,<ref name=":36">{{Cite news |title=Messi, Salah & Co.: Die ESM-Topelf der Saison |url=https://www.kicker.de/messi-salah-26-co-_die-esm-topelf-der-saison-725703/slideshow |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260106090401/https://www.kicker.de/messi-salah-26-co-_die-esm-topelf-der-saison-725703/slideshow |archive-date=6 January 2026 |access-date=26 June 2026 |work=kicker |language=de-DE}}</ref> and for the second consecutive season was recognised as the best-performing player in Europe according to ''WhoScored'', recording the highest ever rating for a single season in one of Europe's top five leagues up to that point.<ref name="WhoScoredPSG">{{Cite web |title=A waste of time? In the [[2018–19 UEFA Champions League|Champions League]] group stage, Neymar helped PSG top a group that included the previous year's runners-up [[Liverpool F.C.|Liverpool]], as well as [[SSC Napoli|Napoli]] and [[Red Star Belgrade]].<ref name="UEFAErrors" /> He scored a hattrick in a 6–1 win over Red Star Belgrade (including two free kicks) and PSG's second goal in their 2–1 win against Liverpool, which meant he surpassed Kaká's tally of 30 goals to become the top-scoring Brazilian in the Champions League with 31 goals.<ref>{{Cite web |date=28 November 2018 |title=PSG news: Neymar On 19 January 2019, Neymar provided two goals and an assist in PSG's 9–0 defeat of Guingamp, the club's biggest home win.<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/report/_/gameId/512173 |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN |title=PSG 9-0 Guingamp (19 Jan, 2019) Game Analysis - ESPN (UK) |archive-date=18 October 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20251018065945/https://www.espn.co.uk/football/report/_/gameId/512173 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=19 January 2019 |title=PSG destroy Guingamp 9-0 |url=https://www.rfi.fr/en/sports/20190119-psg-destroy-guingamp-9-0 |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=Radio France Internationale |language=en |archive-date=14 December 2019 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20191214141813/http://www.rfi.fr/en/sports/20190119-psg-destroy-guingamp-9-0 |url-status=live }}</ref> On 23 January, nearly a year after his season-ending injury, Neymar reinjured the metatarsal in his right foot in PSG's win against [[RC Strasbourg Alsace|Strasbourg]] in the Coupe de France.<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/report/_/gameId/533415 |access-date=4 June 2026 |publisher=ESPN|title=PSG 2-0 Strasbourg (23 Jan, 2019) Game Analysis - ESPN (UK)}}</ref> He missed the Champions League round of 16 tie against [[Manchester United F.C.|Manchester United]]; after United eliminated PSG, Neymar went on social media to insult video review officials for awarding a stoppage-time penalty to United, resulting in [[UEFA]] banning him for three matches in UEFA competitions for the insult.<ref name="APgoad">{{cite web | url=https://www.apnews.com/f36fd5d49a0e4e6f83f3f5fb3ee3f7f5 | first=Jerome | last=Pugmire | title=Neymar appears to aim punch at fan goading him after defeat | date=28 April 2019 | url-status=live | archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201219103441/https://apnews.com/f36fd5d49a0e4e6f83f3f5fb3ee3f7f5 | archive-date=19 December 2020|access-date=4 May 2019|website=[[Associated Press]]}}</ref><ref>{{cite news|last=Mather|first=Victor|date=28 January 2019|title=P.S.G.'s Neymar Joins Growing Champions League Injury List|work=The New York Times|url=https://www.nytimes.com/2019/01/28/sports/neymar-champions-league-psg.html|url-status=live|access-date=8 March 2019|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201108010844/https://www.nytimes.com/2019/01/28/sports/neymar-champions-league-psg.html|archive-date=8 November 2020}}</ref> 

On 27 April, Neymar returned from the foot injury to play in the [[2019 Coupe de France final]] against Rennes, where he assisted the opening goal and scored PSG's second, but Rennes made a comeback and won on penalties. After the match, he was caught on video appearing to punch a spectator in the face, who had been filming and insulting PSG players.<ref name="APgoad" /><ref name="goalpunch2">{{cite web |last1=Bairner|first1=Robin|date=1 May 2019|title=Will Neymar be banned for fan punch? May 2019|access-date=4 May 2019|website=[[Goal (website)|Goal]]}}</ref> Although PSG had already clinched the [[2018–19 Ligue 1|Ligue 1]] title on 21 April, Neymar continued to contribute in the league.<ref>{{Cite news |date=21 April 2019 |title=European round-up: PSG crowned Ligue 1 champions after Lille draw |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2019/apr/21/psg-crowned-ligue-1-champions-lille-draw-toulouse-european-roundup-dortmund |access-date=28 July 2026 |work=The Guardian |language=en-GB |issn=0261-3077}}</ref> On 4 May, he scored the equaliser from the penalty spot in a 1–1 draw with Nice.<ref>{{Cite news |date=4 May 2019 |title=Paris Saint-Germain 1-1 Nice: Cavani misses late penalty for champions |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/48153872 |access-date=28 July 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB scoring with a diving header before assisting PSG's second goal in a 2–1 win against [[Angers SCO|Angers]].<ref>{{Cite news |date=11 May 2019 |title=Angers 1-2 Paris St-Germain: Neymar helps champions end away losing streak |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/48226956 |access-date=28 July 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB actions in the Coupe de France final, causing him to miss the final two league games of the season and the eventual [[2019 Trophée des Champions]].<ref>{{Cite web |last=Petrequin |first=Samuel Despite the cup final disappointment and another injury interrupted season, Neymar trailed only Mbappé in PSG's top goalscorers of the season, with 23 goals in 28 matches.<ref>{{Cite web |date=23 May ==== 2019–20: Decisive in Ligue 1, Coupe de France final winning goal and UEFA Champions League runner-up ====
In July 2019, it was revealed that Neymar wanted to leave PSG as he felt referees never protected him, there was jealousy towards him from some dressing room team-mates, fans did not appreciate his talent showcased in PSG and sections of the French media were against him.<ref name=":9">{{Cite web |title=Neymar: Why did he choose to remain at Paris Saint-Germain? |url=https://www.skysports.com/app/transfer/news/11820/11801047/neymar-why-did-he-choose-to-remain-at-paris-saint-germain |access-date=20 November 2023 |website=SkySports |archive-date=20 November 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231120180409/https://www.skysports.com/app/transfer/news/11820/11801047/neymar-why-did-he-choose-to-remain-at-paris-saint-germain |url-status=live}}</ref> He heavily pushed for a move back to Barcelona, and was also linked with Real Madrid.<ref name=":10" /><ref>{{Cite web |date=2022-03-06 |title=Neymar nearly signed for Real Madrid in 2019 |url=https://e01-marca.uecdn.es/en/football/psg/2022/03/06/6224b8ceca4741ea398b45c6.html |access-date=2026-09-30 |website=MARCA |language=en}}</ref> He ultimately agreed to stay at PSG after talks with Barcelona broke down.<ref name=":10">{{cite news |date=1 September 2019 |title=Neymar: Paris Saint-Germain forward agrees to stay after Barcelona talks break down |work=Sky Sports |url=https://www.skysports.com/football/news/11820/11799320/neymar-paris-saint-germain-forward-agrees-to-stay-after-barcelona-talks-break-down |url-status=live |access-date=6 September 2019 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201107233653/https://www.skysports.com/football/news/11820/11799320/neymar-paris-saint-germain-forward-agrees-to-stay-after-barcelona-talks-break-down |archive-date=7 November 2020}}</ref><ref name=":9" />  [[File:Neymar vs Lille (cropped).jpg|thumb|220x220px|Neymar before converting his penalty against [[Lille OSC|Lille]] in Ligue 1 in January 2020]]The long drawn-out transfer speculation of Neymar returning to Barcelona caused tensions in Paris.<ref name=":34">{{Cite web |last=Klosok |first=Aleks |date=11 August 2020 |title=Why it's now or never for Neymar |url=https://www.cnn.com/2020/08/11/football/neymar-psg-champions-league-brazil-cmd-spt-intl |access-date=8 June 2025 |publisher=CNN |archive-date=16 November 2022 first appearance for PSG in the [[2019–20 Paris Saint-Germain F.C. season|2019–20 season]], he was relentlessly booed throughout the match before scoring a [[bicycle kick]] in the 92nd minute to win 1–0 against Strasbourg in Ligue 1.<ref name=":34" /><ref>{{cite news|date=14 September 2019 |title=Last-gasp win!|publisher=Paris Saint-Germain F.C.|url=https://en.psg.fr/teams/first-team/content/last-gasp-win|url-status=live|access-date=15 September 2019 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200710000245/https://en.psg.fr/teams/first-team/content/last-gasp-win|archive-date=10 July 2020}}</ref> He scored another late winner on the next matchday as PSG defeated [[Olympique Lyonnais|Lyon]] 1–0 on 23 September.<ref>{{cite web|date=23 September 2019 |title=Lyon 0–1 Paris St-Germain|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/49783196|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200814174359/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/49783196|archive-date=14 August 2020|access-date=25 July 2020| website=BBC}}</ref> Neymar continued to be vital in Ligue 1, scoring the only goal in a win against Bordeaux,<ref>{{Cite web |date=28 September 2019 |title=Neymar is PSG's hero again |url=https://www.theboltonnews.co.uk/sport/nationalsport/17934266.neymar-psgs-hero/ |access-date=19 May 2026 |website=The Bolton News |language=en}}</ref> scoring and assisting in a 3–1 comeback win against [[Montpellier HSC|Montpellier]],<ref name=":27">{{Cite web |title=Neymar Jr. Official |url=https://www.neymarjr.com/ |access-date=6 June 2025 |website=Neymar Jr. |archive-date=8 November 2020 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201108131926/https://www.neymarjr.com/ |url-status=live }}</ref> and assisting twice and scoring in a 4–0 win against [[AS 47 goals in his first 50 matches in Ligue 1.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Marsden |first=Rory |title=Neymar's 7-Match Scoring Streak Ends as PSG Defeats Montpellier Entering into the new year, Neymar continued his fine form for PSG. He was at the core of all of PSG's goals against Monaco in Ligue 1 on 12 January 2020, scoring twice and causing an own goal, though the match ended in a 3–3 draw.<ref>{{Cite web |title=PSG 3-3 Monaco (12 Jan, 2020) Final Score - ESPN (UK) |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/542035/as-monaco-paris-saint-germain |access-date=2026-10-27 |website=ESPN |language=en}}</ref> Two weeks later, on 26 January, he scored both goals in a 2–0 win against Lille.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Lille 0-2 PSG (Jan 26, 2020) Game Analysis |url=https://www.espn.com/soccer/report/_/gameId/542030 |access-date=3 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN |language=en |archive-date=16 August 2024 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240816052920/https://www.espn.com/soccer/report/_/gameId/542030 |url-status=live }}</ref> On 4 March, Neymar converted from the penalty spot to put PSG 2–1 up against Lyon in the [[2019–20 Coupe de France]] semi-finals; PSG went on to win 5–1.<ref>{{Cite web |last=semifinal |first=A. ==== 2020–21: Goalscoring records, Champions League heroics and Ligue 1 title race ====
Early in [[2020–21 Paris Saint-Germain FC season|2020–21]], Neymar entered [[List of Paris Saint-Germain F.C. records and statistics#All-time top scorers|PSG's top ten goalscorers]] as he scored his 72nd goal for the club in a 6–1 win against Angers on October 3, in which he scored twice and assisted a goal.<ref name=":27"/> In November, he reached 50 goals in Ligue 1 in 58 matches, making him the fastest player in the 21st century and the third-fastest player ever to do so, surpassing the likes of forwards [[Zlatan Ibrahimović]] and [[Radamel Falcao]].<ref>{{Cite web |last=century |first=((Neymar becomes the fastest player to score 50 Ligue 1 goals in the 21st)) |title=Neymar Fastest To 50 Ligue 1 Goals |url=https://www.beinsports.com/en-us/soccer/ligue-1/articles-video/neymar-fastest-to-50-ligue-1-goals |access-date=7 August 2026 |website=beIN SPORTS |language=en-us |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260807213653/https://www.beinsports.com/en-us/soccer/ligue-1/articles-video/neymar-fastest-to-50-ligue-1-goals |archive-date=7 August 2026 |url-status=live}}</ref> Like the previous season, he was largely influential for PSG in the [[2020–21 UEFA Champions League|Champions League]], where they lost two of their opening three games in the group stage, being at risk of being eliminated.{{cn|date=November 2026}} In PSG's final three games in their group, Neymar converted a penalty as his side won 1–0 over Leipzig, scored twice in their 3–1 victory against Manchester United at [[Old Trafford]], and netted a hat-trick in their 5–1 win against [[İstanbul Başakşehir F.K.|İstanbul Başakşehir]].<ref>{{Cite web|date=9 December 2020|title=Hat-trick: Neymar Jr's gala performance on the Champions League|url=https://www.neymarjr.com/en/football/hat-trick-neymar-jr-gala-performance-on-the-champions-league|access-date=10 December 2020|website=neymarjr.com|archive-date=14 January 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210114150953/https://www.neymarjr.com/en/football/hat-trick-neymar-jr-gala-performance-on-the-champions-league|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |last=Morse |first=Ben |date=2 December 2020 |title=Neymar masterclass rips apart Manchester United as PSG is victorious in Champions League |url=https://www.cnn.com/2020/12/02/football/neymar-psg-manchester-united-ucl-spt-intl |access-date=30 June 2025 |publisher=CNN |language=en |archive-date=30 June 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250630085715/https://www.cnn.com/2020/12/02/football/neymar-psg-manchester-united-ucl-spt-intl |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/585803/rb-leipzig-paris-saint-germain#:~:text=PSG%201-0%20RB%20Leipzig,Final%20Score%20-%20ESPN%20(UK) |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN |title=PSG 1-0 RB Lepizig (24 Nov, 2020) Final Score - ESPN (UK) |archive-date=24 August 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250824115335/https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/585803/rb-leipzig-paris-saint-germain#:~:text=PSG%201-0%20RB%20Leipzig,Final%20Score%20-%20ESPN%20(UK) |url-status=live }}</ref> His goals made him the first player in [[European Cup]] and Champions League history to reach 20 goals for two different clubs, having previously done so with Barcelona.<ref name=":51">{{Cite web|last=Zavala|first=Steve|date=10 December 2020|title=Neymar is the First Player Ever to Score 20 Champions League Goals for Two Different Teams|url=https://psgtalk.com/2020/12/neymar-is-the-first-player-ever-to-score-20-champions-league-goals-for-two-different-teams/|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201210101132/https://psgtalk.com/2020/12/neymar-is-the-first-player-ever-to-score-20-champions-league-goals-for-two-different-teams/|archive-date=10 December 2020|access-date=10 December 2020|website=PSG Talk}}</ref> He also became the fourth-quickest player to reach 40 goals in the Champions League (65 matches).<ref name="UEFAErrors"/> His third goal against Başakşehir was named by UEFA as the best goal of the 2020–21 Champions League group stage.<ref name=":272">{{Cite web |title=Neymar Jr. Official|url=https://www.neymarjr.com/|access-date=6 June 2025|website=Neymar Jr.|archive-date=8 November 2020|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201108131926/https://www.neymarjr.com/|url-status=live}}</ref>

Ahead of the semi-final, the duo of Neymar and Mbappé was viewed as the main threat to Man City.<ref>Sources for duo recognition:

In May, towards the end of the season, Neymar was crucial in Ligue 1 amid the title race with Lille, scoring a goal and assisting another in a 2–1 victory over [[RC Lens|Lens]] to return PSG back to the top of the league table.<ref>{{Cite web |date=1 May 2021 |title=Paris Saint-Germain 2-1 Lens: Neymar leads champions to significant win |url=https://www.fotmob.com/news/19ungzt73kt3r1o0uqj5eeuwtd/paris-saint-germain-2-1-lens-neymar-leads-champions-to-significant-win |access-date=18 May 2026 |website=FotMob |language=en}}</ref> He went on to open the scoring against Rennes with a penalty in an eventual 1–1 draw and opened the scoring with a penalty again in a 4–0 win over [[Stade de Reims|Reims]].<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/team-stats/_/gameId/572911#:~:text=PSG%204-0%20Reims%20(16,Team%20Stats%20-%20ESPN%20(UK) |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN|title=PSG 4-0 Reims (16 May, 2021) Final Score - ESPN (UK)}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/572922/paris-saint-germain-stade-rennais |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN|title=Rennes 1-1 PSG (9 May, 2021) Final Score - ESPN (UK)}}</ref> On 23 May, however, Lille pipped PSG to the title by just one point, preventing them from winning a record-tying 10th league title.<ref>{{Cite news |date=24 May 2021 |title=Ligue 1: How Lille pulled off a shock title triumph - to leave PSG searching for answers |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/57224119 |access-date=18 May 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB |archive-date=30 May 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210530224747/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/57224119 |url-status=live }}</ref> Despite this, they won the [[2020–21 Coupe de France|Coupe de France]], which was Neymar's third and final cup with PSG.<ref>{{cite news |date=19 May 2021 |title=Kylian Mbappé stars as PSG beat Monaco in Coupe de France final |url=https://www.ligue1.com/Articles/OTHER-COMPETITIONS/2021/05/19/kylian-mbappe-stars-as-psg-beat-monaco-in-coupe-de-france-final |access-date=21 May 2021 |website=ligue1.com |archive-date=21 May 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210521223046/https://www.ligue1.com/Articles/OTHER-COMPETITIONS/2021/05/19/kylian-mbappe-stars-as-psg-beat-monaco-in-coupe-de-france-final |url-status=live }}</ref> 

Neymar was nominated for the [[2021 Ballon d'Or]], which was his ninth nomination — a joint record for a Brazilian player.<ref name=":27" /> At the end of the season, the CIES Football Observatory, with support of performance data by [[InStat]], concluded that Neymar ranked second in their list of the top 10 players in Europe during 2020–21 (ahead of Lewandowski and only behind Messi), simultaneously being recognised as the best-performing player in Ligue 1.<ref name=":58">{{Cite web |title=InStat Index rankings: the top 10s across Europe - CIES Football Observatory |url=https://football-observatory.com/InStat-Index-rankings-the-top-10s-across-Europe-2632 |access-date=2026-09-11 |website=football-observatory.com}}</ref><ref name=":64">{{Cite web |last=Sagorin |first=Yannick |date=3 May 2021 |title=Stats à l'appui, qui sont les meilleurs joueurs d'Europe ? |url=https://www.sports.fr/football/divers/stats-a-lappui-meilleurs-joueurs-deurope-457276.html |access-date=5 August 2026 |website=Sports.fr |language=fr-FR}}</ref> Earlier on in the year, the [[IFFHS]] ranked him only behind Messi, Ronaldo, and [[Andrés Iniesta]] for their [[IFFHS World's Best Player#The Best Man Player of the Decade (2011–2020)|World's Best Player of the Decade]] award.<ref name=":44">{{Cite web |date=8 June 2025 |title=Neymar Finished at 4th Behind Messi, Ronaldo and Iniesta for the IFFHS World's Best Player of the Decade Honor |url=https://onefootball.com/en/news/neymar-finished-at-4th-behind-messi-ronaldo-and-iniesta-for-the-iffhs-worlds-best-player-of-the-decade-honor-32344574 |access-date=8 June 2025 |website=OneFootball }}</ref>

On 8 May, Neymar extended his contract with PSG until 2025.<ref>{{Cite web |date=8 May 2021 |title=PSG's Neymar signs contract extension |url=https://www.espn.com/soccer/story/_/id/37617017/psg-neymar-signs-contract-extension-2025 |access-date=8 May 2021 |publisher=ESPN |archive-date=8 May 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210508171015/https://www.espn.com/soccer/soccer-transfers/story/4379755/psgs-neymar-signs-contract-extension-to-2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> Upon this extension, he said he wanted to achieve PSG's biggest dream, to win the Champions League.<ref name=":24">{{Cite news |date=8 May 2021 |title='I am happy and proud': Neymar commits future to PSG until 2025 |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2021/may/08/neymar-commits-future-to-psg-signs-contract-until-2025 |access-date=5 June 2025 |work=The Guardian |issn=0261-3077}}</ref>

==== 2021–23: 100th club goal, return to top form and departure ====
[[File:Mbappe Messi Neymar.jpg|thumb|250x250px|Neymar (right) alongside Messi and [[Kylian Mbappé]] in a league fixture against [[Olympique de Marseille|Marseille]] (''[[Le Classique]]'') in October 2021]]
Ahead of the [[2021–22 Paris Saint-Germain F.C. season|2021–22 season]], PSG had acquired several new players, including Neymar's former teammate Messi and longtime Real Madrid defender [[Sergio Ramos]], leading the club to be considered genuine favourites to win the [[2021–22 UEFA Champions League|2021–22 Champions League]].<ref>{{Cite web |author1=Mark White |date=28 September 2021 |title=Champions League 2021/22 favourites: Who has the best squad in Europe? |url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/features/champions-league-2021-22-favourites-best-squads-liverpool-man-utd-chelsea-real-madrid-barcelona-psg |access-date=31 May 2026 |website=FourFourTwo |language=en}}</ref> The arrival of Messi also saw the formation of a new attacking trio of Mbappé, Neymar, and Messi, dubbed by the media as ''MNM''.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Onrubia |first=Andrés |date=26 October 2022 |title=MNM - no stopping PSG's terrific trio |url=https://en.as.com/soccer/mnm-no-stopping-psgs-terrific-trio-n/ |access-date=31 May 2026 |website=AS USA |language=en-us |archive-date=10 May 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260510121237/https://en.as.com/soccer/mnm-no-stopping-psgs-terrific-trio-n/ |url-status=live }}</ref>

Neymar scored his first goal of the season on 19 September, converting a penalty in a 2–1 win over Lyon.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Player Ratings {{!}} PSG 2-1 Lyon, Neymar's brilliance and a questionable penalty decision give PSG a barely deserved win|url=https://www.getfootballnewsfrance.com/2021/player-ratings-psg-2-1-lyon-neymars-brilliance-and-a-questionable-penalty-decision-give-psg-a-barely-deserved-win/|access-date=20 September 2021|website=Get French Football News|date=19 September 2021|archive-date=20 September 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210920223408/https://www.getfootballnewsfrance.com/2021/player-ratings-psg-2-1-lyon-neymars-brilliance-and-a-questionable-penalty-decision-give-psg-a-barely-deserved-win/|url-status=live}}</ref> He scored his 400th career goal on 6 November against Bordeaux, where he scored a brace and helped the team win the match 3–2.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Neymar nets 400th career goal in Paris Saint-Germain win over Bordeaux|url=https://www.espn.com/soccer/report/_/gameId/609408|access-date=8 November 2021|publisher=ESPN|archive-date=8 November 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211108072441/https://www.espn.com/soccer/report/_/gameId/609408|url-status=live}}</ref> On 28 November, Neymar suffered an ankle injury in a 3–1 win against Saint-Étienne.<ref name=":75">{{Cite web |date=29 November 2021 |title=Neymar: PSG forward out six to eight weeks with ankle injury suffered vs St Etienne |url=https://www.skysports.com/football/news/12481410/neymar-psg-forward-out-six-to-eight-weeks-with-ankle-injury-suffered-vs-st-etienne |access-date=3 October 2026 |website=Sky Sports}}</ref> The next day, PSG announced that Neymar would be unavailable for up to eight weeks.<ref name=":75" />

On 15 February 2022, Neymar returned in the round of 16 first leg in the Champions League as PSG faced Real Madrid.<ref name=":76">{{Cite web |title=Neymar Jr. Official |url=https://www.neymarjr.com/ |access-date=2026-10-03 |website=Neymar Jr. |language=en}}</ref> He came off the bench in the second half and assisted Mbappé with a backheel pass to help his team win 1–0.<ref name=":76" /> On 3 April, Neymar scored twice in a 5–1 victory against [[FC Lorient|Lorient]] before scoring a hat-trick six days later on 9 April, as PSG defeated [[Clermont Foot 63|Clermont]] 6–1.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Clermont Foot 1-6 PSG (9 Apr, 2022) Final Score - ESPN (UK) |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/609227/paris-saint-germain-clermont-foot |access-date=24 April 2026 |publisher=ESPN |language=en |archive-date=27 April 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260427002027/https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/609227/paris-saint-germain-clermont-foot |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/609236/lorient-paris-saint-germain#:~:text=PSG%20v%20Lorient&text=Kylian%20Mbappe%20was%20devastating%20on,Lorient%205-1%20on%20Sunday. |url-status=live }}</ref> On 21 May, he scored his 100th goal for PSG in a 5–0 win over [[FC Metz|Metz]], making him one of the few players to have scored at least 100 goals for three different clubs.<ref>{{cite news |title=Neymar emulates Ronaldo & Romario after bringing up 100 goals for PSG |access-date=27 May 2022 |website=Goal}}</ref><ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/61537573 |title=Paris Saint Germain 5–0 Metz |website=BBC Sport |date=21 May 2022 |access-date=21 record-tying 10th Ligue 1 title, though with 13 goals and 8 assists across all competitions, this was his lowest output since arriving in Europe in 2013.<ref>{{cite web fewest goals since arriving in Europe |date=23 May 2022 |access-date=18 August 2022 |work=UOL}}{{Dead link|date=September 2023|bot=InternetArchiveBot|fix-attempted=yes}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/4179531/2022/04/22/psg-crowned-ligue-1-champions-after-messi-secures-draw-against-lens/ |access-date=23 April 2022 |website=The Athletic |archive-date=27 February Neymar drew criticism for his decline in performance compared to his previous seasons.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Laurens |first=Julien |date=2 November 2021 |title=What's happened to Neymar? PSG No. 10 is in worst form since joining Ligue 1 giants |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37622121/happened-neymar-psg-no-10-worst-form-joining-ligue-1-giants |access-date=28 April 2026
