notably scored just three goals in Ligue 1 by the time of the lengthy injury sustained in November and scored no goals in the entire Champions League campaign for the first time in his career.<ref>{{Cite news |date=29 November 2021 |title=Neymar: Paris St-Germain forward suffers ankle ligament injury |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/59454639 |access-date=29 May 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB |archive-date=21 December 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20251221004425/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/59454639 |url-status=live }}</ref> In the previous year, he suggested that he was growing tired of the intense demands of European football.<ref name=":2">{{Cite web |date=7 March 2022 |title=Henry worried about Neymar's mental health as PSG star continues to underwhelm {{!}} Goal.com UK |url=https://www.goal.com/en-gb/news/henry-worried-neymar-mental-health-psg/blt49ccc50c31e743ad |access-date=28 April 2026 |website=Goal |language=en-GB}}</ref> He was linked with a potential departure from PSG, but ultimately remained at the club.<ref name="Newseason">{{cite web|last1=Banerjee|first1=Apratim|date=2 September 2022|title=PSG wanted to sell Neymar and sign Barcelona superstar to play alongside Kylian Mbappe this summer: Reports|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/football/news-psg-wanted-sell-neymar-sign-barcelona-superstar-play-alongside-kylian-mbappe-summer-reports|access-date=16 November 2022|website=Sportskeeda|archive-date=16 November 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221116104532/https://www.sportskeeda.com/football/news-psg-wanted-sell-neymar-sign-barcelona-superstar-play-alongside-kylian-mbappe-summer-reports|url-status=live}}</ref> Ahead of the new season, he said his ambitions included winning the Champions League, further saying, “It must be with Paris. I have a contract with PSG, so there is no other choice.”<ref>{{Cite web |date=24 April 2026 |title=Neymar Shares Early Goals for 2022-2023 Season With PSG |url=https://onefootball.com/en/news/neymar-shares-early-goals-for-2022-2023-season-with-psg-35190023 |access-date=24 April 2026 |website=OneFootball |language=en |archive-date=24 April 2026 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20260424150328/https://onefootball.com/en/news/neymar-shares-early-goals-for-2022-2023-season-with-psg-35190023 |url-status=live }}</ref>[[File:Messi Neymar Mbappé PSG 2022 (cropped).jpg|thumb|275x275px|Neymar celebrating with Mbappé after scoring in Ligue 1 in August 2022]]
Neymar started the [[2022–23 Paris Saint-Germain FC season|2022–23 season]] in tremendous form, scoring twice against Nantes on 31 July in PSG's 4–0 result to win the Trophée des Champions, scoring a goal and providing a hat-trick of assists in a 5–0 win against Clermont on 6 August, scoring twice in a 5–2 win against Montpellier, and scoring twice and registering three more assists in a 7–1 away win over Lille.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Grez |first=Matias |date=22 August 2022 |title=Kylian Mbappé scores a hat-trick – including 8-second goal from kick-off – as Paris Saint-Germain puts seven goals past Lille |url=https://edition.cnn.com/2022/08/22/football/kylian-mbappe-hat-trick-psg-hammers-lille-spt-intl/index.html |access-date=27 September 2022 |publisher=[[CNN]] |archive-date=29 September 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220929134307/https://edition.cnn.com/2022/08/22/football/kylian-mbappe-hat-trick-psg-hammers-lille-spt-intl/index.html |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite news |date=6 August 2022 |title=Clermont 0-5 Paris St-Germain: Neymar scores and sets up three more in dominant win |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/62452205 |access-date=28 May 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB |archive-date=2 December 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221202000731/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/62452205 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=31 July 2022 |title=A final, lots of goals and a trophy: a great start to the season! |url=https://en.psg.fr/teams/first-team/content/a-final-lots-of-goals-and-a-trophy-a-great-start-to-the-season-match-report-paris-saint-germain-trophee-des-champions-psg-nantes |access-date=1 August 2022 |publisher=[[Paris Saint-Germain F.C.]] |archive-date=14 August 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220814123500/https://en.psg.fr/teams/first-team/content/a-final-lots-of-goals-and-a-trophy-a-great-start-to-the-season-match-report-paris-saint-germain-trophee-des-champions-psg-nantes |url-status=dead }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/639622/montpellier-paris-saint-germain |access-date=28 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN |title=PSG 5-2 Montpellier (13 Aug, 2022) Final Score - ESPN (UK) |archive-date=5 May 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250505153757/https://www.espn.co.uk/football/match/_/gameId/639622/montpellier-paris-saint-germain |url-status=live }}</ref> He was named Ligue 1's [[UNFP Player of the Month|Player of the Month]] in August.<ref name="Newseason" /><ref name="unfp.org">{{cite web |date=22 September 2022 |title=Neymar Jr, joueur du mois d'août de la Ligue 1 Uber Eats ! |trans-title=Neymar Jr, player of the month for August in Ligue 1 Uber Eats! |url=https://www.unfp.org/2022/09/neymar-jr-joueur-du-mois-daout-de-la-ligue-1-uber-eats/ |access-date=24 September 2022 |publisher=National Union of Professional Footballers |language=fr |archive-date=5 October 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221005124224/https://www.unfp.org/2022/09/neymar-jr-joueur-du-mois-daout-de-la-ligue-1-uber-eats/ |url-status=live }}</ref> 

On 10 September, Neymar surpassed PSG's former striker [[Pauleta]] to become the club's fourth-top scorer of all time, scoring his 110th goal in a 1–0 win in the league against [[Stade Brestois 29|Brest]].<ref>{{Cite news |date=10 September 2022 |title=Paris St-Germain 1-0 Brest: Neymar scores before Islam Slimani penalty saved |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/62863676 |access-date=4 May 2026 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB}}</ref> On 16 October, he scored in a 1–0 win against Marseille, taking PSG three points clear at the top of the league table.{{cn|date=November 2026}} Neymar helped PSG extend their lead over Lens at the top of the table, scoring the goal to put PSG 3–2 ahead in an eventual 4–3 victory against [[ES Troyes AC|Troyes]], while also assisting a goal.<ref>{{Cite news |date=2022-10-29 |title=Paris St-Germain 4-3 Troyes: Lionel Messi, Neymar and Kylian Mbappe all score in PSG's win |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/63442631 |access-date=2026-09-11 |work=BBC Sport |language=en-GB}}</ref> Neymar was influential in Ligue 1 again on 8 November, when he produced a goal and an assist to help PSG defeat Lorient, winning 2–1.<ref name=":27" />

In the group stage of the [[2022–23 UEFA Champions League|Champions League]], Neymar registered 2 goals and 2 assists against Juventus, [[Maccabi Haifa F.C.|Maccabi Haifa]] and [[S.L. Benfica|Benfica]], helping PSG advance to the knockout stage.<ref name="UEFAErrors"/><ref>{{Cite web |title=Neymar 22/23 Stats |url=https://www.statmuse.com/fc/ask?q=Neymar+22%2F23+stats&l=ucl |access-date=28 July 2026 |website=StatMuse |language=en}}</ref> Neymar had returned to his best form and was considered the most in-form player in the world, scoring 15 goals and providing 11 assists in just 20 games.<ref name=":11" /><ref name=":28">{{Cite web |title=PSG-Auxerre preview: 'best ever Neymar' to play? |url=https://www.ligue1.com/Articles/News/2022/11/10/psg-auxerre-preview-best-ever-neymar-to-play |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231120102320/https://www.ligue1.com/Articles/News/2022/11/10/psg-auxerre-preview-best-ever-neymar-to-play |archive-date=20 November 2023 |access-date=20 November 2023 |website=Ligue1 COM }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |title=Ranking the 5 favorites for the 2023 Ballon d'Or{{!}} All Football |url=https://m.allfootballapp.com/news/EPL/Ranking-the-5-favorites-for-the-2023-Ballon-dOr/2907575 |access-date=29 April 2026 |website=AllfootballOfficial |language=en}}</ref><ref name=":65">{{Cite web |last=Ryan |first=Danny |date=20 December 2022 |title=Messi, Mbappe, Neymar: Who is the favourite for the 2023 Ballon d'Or? |url=https://www.givemesport.com/88096487-messi-mbappe-neymar-who-is-the-favourite-for-the-2023-ballon-dor/ |access-date=29 April 2026 |website=GiveMeSport |language=en}}</ref> Ahead of the [[2022 FIFA World Cup|2022 World Cup]] that would take place during the winter, both English football pundits [[Jamie Redknapp]] and [[Gary Neville]] tipped him to be the star player of the tournament.<ref name="SkyWC2022">{{Cite web |last=Grounds |first=Ben |date=24 November 2022 |title=World Cup 2022: Neymar dependency lingers but Brazil have temperament and quality to rule in Qatar |url=https://www.skysports.com/football/news/12027/12719401/world-cup-2022-neymar-dependency-lingers-but-brazil-have-temperament-and-quality-to-rule-in-qatar |website=Sky Sports}}</ref> He was also an early favourite for the [[2023 Ballon d'Or]].<ref name=":65" /> In January 2023, Neymar was announced as one of the fourteen players nominated for the [[The Best FIFA Football Awards 2022#The Best FIFA Men's Player|2022 The Best FIFA Men's Player]] award.<ref name="nominees">{{cite web|url=https://www.fifa.com/fifaplus/en/articles/the-best-fifa-mens-player-award-messi-alvarez-neymar-salah-mane-mbappe-bellingham-benzema-debruyne-haaland-vinicius-lewandowski-hakimi-modric|title=The Best FIFA Men's Player: Nominees in focus|website=FIFA.com|publisher=Fédération Internationale de Football Association|date=12 January 2023|access-date=12 January 2023}}</ref>
[[File:Cassel vs PSG (Coupe de France 2023).jpg|left|thumb|Neymar during a [[2022–23 Coupe de France|Coupe de France]] match against [[US Pays de Cassel|Pays de Cassel]] in January 2023]]
On 19 February, Neymar suffered a season-ending ankle injury against Lille in a Ligue 1 fixture, in which he scored PSG's second goal in a dramatic 4–3 comeback victory.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Espn |date=19 February 2023 |title=Neymar stretchered off in tears with ankle injury; faces race to play in Champions League tie |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37636464/neymar-stretchered-tears-ankle-injury-faces-race-play-champions-league-tie |access-date=29 April 2026 |publisher=ESPN |language=en}}</ref><ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/64868512 |title=Paris St-Germain striker to have ankle surgery and set to be out for the rest of the season |website=BBC |date=6 March 2023 |accessdate=6 March 2023 |archive-date=6 March 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230306173247/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/football/64868512 |url-status=live}}</ref> Following PSG's elimination by Bayern Munich in the Champions League round of 16 on 8 March, Neymar and Messi faced heavy criticism from sections of the French media and PSG supporters; although he missed the second leg, his performance in the first leg — along with other PSG players — was criticised.<ref>{{Cite web |date=8 March 2023 |title=PSG Deserving of Another Early Champions League Exit With Heartless Display |url=https://www.si.com/soccer/2023/03/08/psg-champions-league-elimination-bayern-munich-video |access-date=2 August 2026 |website=SI |language=en-US}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |title=Vos notes de PSG-Bayern : Neymar et Soler dézingués, Ramos épargné |url=https://www.lequipe.fr/Football/Actualites/Vos-notes-de-psg-bayern-neymar-et-soler-dezingues-ramos-epargne/1380884 |access-date=2 August 2026 |website=L'Équipe |language=fr}}</ref> On 27 May, PSG won Ligue 1 by just one point ahead of Lens; this was Neymar's fifth and final league title in France, contributing with 13 goals and 11 assists in 20 matches.<ref>{{Cite web |date=27 May 2023 |title=Football : le PSG remporte son onzième titre de champion de France |url=https://www.france24.com/fr/info-en-continu/20230527-football-le-psg-remporte-son-onzi%C3%A8me-titre-de-champion-de-france |access-date=7 June 2023 |publisher=[[France 24]] |language=Fr}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |title=Messi, Neymar; Haaland, De Bruyne? Which duo has the most goal contributions in Europe this season? |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/37636795/messi-neymar-haaland-de-bruyne-which-duo-most-goal-contributions-europe-season |access-date=6 August 2026 |website=www.espn.co.uk}}</ref> 

At the end of the season, PSG began a squad overhaul, with Neymar being one of several players not being in the team's plans for the following season.<ref>{{cite web |date=9 August 2023 |title=Neymar is informed by Luis Enrique that PSG does not count on him, says radio |url=https://placar.com.br/futebol-europeu/neymar-e-informado-por-luis-enrique-que-psg-nao-conta-com-ele-diz-radio/ |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230816033608/https://placar.com.br/futebol-europeu/neymar-e-informado-por-luis-enrique-que-psg-nao-conta-com-ele-diz-radio/ |archive-date=16 August 2023 |access-date=17 September 2023 |publisher=Scoreboard}}</ref> Neymar was linked with a potential return to Barcelona, however, Barcelona's hierarchy never reached an advanced stage to discuss finer economic details and possibilities, as the club's manager Xavi was not keen on signing him, leading both clubs to fail to reach an agreement.<ref>{{cite web |date=15 August 2023 |title=Why Barcelona failed to sign Neymar ahead of Al-Hilal |url=https://everythingbarca.com/2023/08/15/barcelona-neymar-al-hilal-xavi/ |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231003201323/https://everythingbarca.com/2023/08/15/barcelona-neymar-al-hilal-xavi/ |archive-date=3 October 2023 |access-date=17 September 2023 |publisher=Everything Barça}}</ref>

Neymar has been credited as a legend of PSG,<ref>{{Cite web |date=21 October 2024 |title=Kylian Mbappe, Zlatan Ibrahimovic & the 25 best PSG players of the 21st century so far - ranked {{!}} Goal.com UK |url=https://www.goal.com/en-gb/lists/kylian-mbappe-zlatan-ibrahimovic-and-the-25-best-psg-players-of-the-21st-century-so-far-ranked/bltb1d271952f7ea8eb |access-date=6 August 2026 |website=www.goal.com |language=en-GB}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=20 June 2025 |title=Best Paris Saint-Germain Players of All Time: Ranking the Top 10 |url=https://www.si.com/soccer/best-paris-saint-germain-players-all-time-ranking-top-10 |access-date=6 August 2026 |website=SI |language=en-US}}</ref> trailing only Ibrahimović, Cavani, and Mbappé in the club's top scorers at the time of his departure, and one of Ligue 1's greatest players.<ref name="ligue1.com">{{Cite web |title=Remembering Neymar: 5 key moments from his PSG career |url=https://www.ligue1.com/Articles/Legends/2023/08/17/remembering-neymar-5-key-moments-from-his-psg-career |access-date=23 November 2023 |website=Ligue1 COM |archive-date=23 November 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231123132015/https://www.ligue1.com/Articles/Legends/2023/08/17/remembering-neymar-5-key-moments-from-his-psg-career |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |last=Churchill |first=Max |date=15 September 2024 |title=10 Greatest Players in Ligue 1 History [Ranked] |url=https://www.givemesport.com/greatest-players-ligue-1-france-football-history-ranked/ |access-date=3 June 2025 |website=GiveMeSport }}</ref><ref name=":33"/> However, his tenure at PSG was marred by injuries, hindering his chances of helping the club win its first Champions League title.<ref>{{Cite web |date=18 October 2023 |title=Neymar's injury hell threatens to derail his career: 16 since 2018 and 640 days sidelined |url=https://www.marca.com/en/football/2023/10/19/653060b322601d9a638b45be.html |access-date=15 November 2023 |website=Marca|location=Spain }}</ref><ref name="FourFourTwo2025">{{Cite web |last=Dabbs |first=Ryan |date=20 January 2025 |title='I was in a position to win the Ballon d'Or at PSG, but injuries made it difficult - that's hindered my chances': Neymar explains why he missed out on winning prestigious individual award |url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/i-was-in-a-position-to-win-the-ballon-dor-at-psg-but-injuries-made-it-difficult-thats-hindered-my-chances-neymar-explains-why-he-missed-out-on-winning-prestigious-individual-award |access-date=5 July 2025 |website=FourFourTwo |language=en}}</ref> He missed 119 matches for PSG in total as a result of being injured around 20 times across his six-year spell, showing a fragility that was not seen either at Barcelona or Santos.<ref name="Le Monde.fr2">{{Cite news |date=15 August 2023 |title=Neymar leaves PSG: The Brazilian star's turbulent journey in the French capital |url=https://www.lemonde.fr/en/sports/article/2023/08/15/neymar-leaves-psg-the-brazilian-star-s-turbulent-journey-in-the-french-capital_6094563_9.html |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240209183407/https://www.lemonde.fr/en/sports/article/2023/08/15/neymar-leaves-psg-the-brazilian-star-s-turbulent-journey-in-the-french-capital_6094563_9.html |archive-date=9 February 2024 |access-date=12 November 2023 |work=Le Monde }}</ref><ref name=":4">{{cite web |last1=Castro |first1=Jorge |date=29 August 2019 |title=Neymar ahead of Bale for time out through injury |url=https://en.as.com/en/2019/08/29/football/1567075330_718706.html |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200813071016/https://en.as.com/en/2019/08/29/football/1567075330_718706.html |archive-date=13 August 2020 |access-date=27 June 2020 |work=AS.com}}</ref> Despite this, he had the second-most goal contributions for the club during his six seasons there (188), only behind Mbappé.<ref name=":57">{{Cite news |last1=Imber |first1=Leon |last2=Hopkins |first2=Jessica |date=24 June 2025 |title=Neymar extends contract with boyhood club Santos until December |url=https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/6445510/2025/06/24/neymar-santos-contract-extension-december/ |access-date=27 June 2025 |work=The New York Times |language=en-US |issn=0362-4331}}</ref> 

Following PSG's first Champions League title win in [[2024–25 UEFA Champions League|2024–25]], their captain [[Marquinhos]] dedicated the triumph to previous players at the club, including Neymar.<ref>{{Cite web |last=read |first=Emmanuel Mendez·2 min |date=1 June 2025 |title=PSG Captain Sends Special Message for Neymar after Champions League Win |url=https://sports.yahoo.com/article/psg-captain-sends-special-message-215907649.html |access-date=5 August 2026 |website=Yahoo Sports |language=en-US}}</ref>

=== Al Hilal ===
On 15 August 2023, Neymar completed a transfer to [[Saudi Pro League]] club [[Al Hilal SFC|Al Hilal]] on a two-year contract, joining a list of European league players who signed for the club, including [[Rúben Neves]], [[Kalidou Koulibaly]], and [[Sergej Milinković-Savić]].<ref>{{Cite web |title=Which players have signed for the Saudi Pro League? |url=https://www.aljazeera.com/news/2023/7/25/which-players-have-signed-for-the-saudi-pro-league |access-date=8 July 2025 |publisher=Al Jazeera |language=en}}</ref><ref>{{cite web |title=Paris Saint-Germain thank Neymar Jr, a club legend |url=https://en.psg.fr/teams/first-team/content/paris-saint-germain-thank-neymar-jr-a-club-legend |website=[[Paris Saint-Germain F.C.]] |date=15 August 2023 |access-date=15 August 2023 |archive-date=15 August 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230815170036/https://en.psg.fr/teams/first-team/content/paris-saint-germain-thank-neymar-jr-a-club-legend |url-status=live }}</ref><ref name="AJ-Hilal">{{cite news |title=Brazil's Neymar signs for Saudi football club Al Hilal |url=https://www.aljazeera.com/sports/2023/8/15/brazils-neymar-signs-for-saudi-football-club-al-hilal |publisher=[[Al Jazeera English|Al Jazeera]] |date=15 August 2023 |access-date=15 August 2023 |archive-date=27 October 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231027002018/https://www.aljazeera.com/sports/2023/8/15/brazils-neymar-signs-for-saudi-football-club-al-hilal |url-status=live }}</ref> The transfer fee was reported to be worth €90&nbsp;million, making him the most expensive purchase in Saudi Pro League history.<ref name="AJ-Hilal" /> He was also reportedly being paid €150m a year at Al Hilal, six times the amount he earned at PSG.<ref>{{Cite news |title=Neymar - superstar with a legacy unfulfilled? |work=BBC Sport |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/66505930 |access-date=28 October 2023 |archive-date=28 October 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231028102324/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/66505930 |url-status=live }}</ref> According to ''The Independent'', Barcelona was the only European club that showed genuine interest in signing Neymar; other top clubs turned away primarily due to his high salary and the expected transfer fee to sign him.<ref>{{Cite web |date=14 August 2023 |title=Neymar agrees Saudi move after only one European club showed interest |url=https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/football/neymar-saudi-arabia-al-hilal-barcelona-transfer-b2392684.html |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231123111040/https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/football/neymar-saudi-arabia-al-hilal-barcelona-transfer-b2392684.html |archive-date=23 November 2023 |access-date=23 November 2023 |website=The Independent}}</ref>

Some media outlets criticised Neymar for choosing to not remain in a top European league and dubbed him as the 'Prince who never became King' due to leaving Europe without being crowned the world's best player with the Ballon d'Or.<ref name=":62">{{Cite web |last=TB |first=Sai Aswin |date=20 August 2023 |title=Neymar - the Prince who never became King |url=https://thefederal.com/category/sports/neymar-the-prince-who-never-became-the-king-94577 |access-date=27 October 2023 |website=thefederal.com }}</ref><ref name=":72">{{Cite news |last=Akdeniz |first=Tolga |date=15 August 2023 |title=Neymar Junior: The dazzling Crown Prince of Brazil who failed to become King |url=https://www.flashscore.com/news/soccer-champions-league-neymar-junior-the-dazzling-crown-prince-of-brazil-who-failed-to-become-king/KfqRle0l/ |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240131011434/https://www.flashscore.com/news/soccer-champions-league-neymar-junior-the-dazzling-crown-prince-of-brazil-who-failed-to-become-king/KfqRle0l/ |archive-date=31 January 2024 |access-date=31 January 2024 |work=Flashscore}}</ref> However, Neymar declared, "I wouldn't be surprised if the Saudi league is better than the French", in light of fellow star players joining the league, including Ronaldo and [[Karim Benzema]].<ref name=":5">{{Cite web |date=5 October 2023 |title='Clock's ticking': Saudi's $9bn sporting blitz explained, what the point is, and why it's just the start |url=https://www.foxsports.com.au/football/saudi-arabian-football-sport-revolution-explained-which-players-have-signed-how-much-has-nation-spent/news-story/42129df147d6dd15c04cdc29035f1bbc |access-date=15 November 2023 |website=Fox Sports |archive-date=15 November 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231115093156/https://www.foxsports.com.au/football/saudi-arabian-football-sport-revolution-explained-which-players-have-signed-how-much-has-nation-spent/news-story/42129df147d6dd15c04cdc29035f1bbc |url-status=live}}</ref> As a marquee signing, Neymar's transfer to Al Hilal drew comparisons to former Brazil international [[Rivellino]]'s transfer to the club in 1978.<ref name=":5" />

==== 2023–25: First matches before injury and termination of contract ====
[[File:Neymar Jr. with Al Hilal, 3 October 2023 - 08.jpg|thumb|Neymar celebrating his first and only goal for [[Al Hilal SFC|Al Hilal]] in October 2023]]
On 15 September 2023, Neymar made his debut for Al Hilal in the league during a 6–1 thrashing of [[Al-Riyadh SC|Al-Riyadh]], coming off the bench and providing an assist to a goal by fellow Brazilian [[Malcom (footballer)|Malcom]].<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.reuters.com/sports/soccer/neymar-makes-debut-al-hilal-hand-al-riyadh-6-1-thrashing-2023-09-16/ |title=Neymar makes debut as Al-Hilal hand Al-Riyadh 6-1 thrashing |publisher=Reuters |date=16 September 2023 |access-date=16 September 2023 |archive-date=16 September 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230916135131/https://www.reuters.com/sports/soccer/neymar-makes-debut-al-hilal-hand-al-riyadh-6-1-thrashing-2023-09-16/ |url-status=live}}</ref> That same year, on 3 October, Neymar scored his first goal for the club in a 3–0 away win against [[F.C. Nassaji Mazandaran|Nassaji Mazandaran]] in an [[2023–24 AFC Champions League group stage|AFC Champions League group stage]] match.<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.reuters.com/sports/soccer/neymar-target-al-hilal-secure-asian-champions-league-win-2023-10-03/ |title=Neymar on target as Al-Hilal secure Asian Champions League win |website=Reuters |date=3 October 2023 |access-date=3 October 2023 |archive-date=3 October 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231003201331/https://www.reuters.com/sports/soccer/neymar-target-al-hilal-secure-asian-champions-league-win-2023-10-03/ |url-status=live }}</ref> He played five games for his new club before [[Anterior cruciate ligament injury|rupturing his anterior cruciate ligament]] while on international duty in October 2023 and was out for the rest of the season.<ref name="ACL injury">{{cite news |title=Neymar to have surgery after ACL injury |work=BBC Sport |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/67152531 |date=18 October 2023 |access-date=18 October 2023 |archive-date=18 October 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231018205021/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/67152531 |url-status=live }}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=21 January 2024 |title=Renan Lodi replaces Neymar: Al Hilal deregisters him as they run out of space. – Super European League |url=https://supereuropeanleague.com/renan-lodi-replaces-neymar-al-hilal-deregisters-him-as-they-run-out-of-space/ |access-date=21 January 2024 |archive-date=9 February 2024 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240209183403/https://supereuropeanleague.com/renan-lodi-replaces-neymar-al-hilal-deregisters-him-as-they-run-out-of-space/ |url-status=live }}</ref> Al Hilal won the [[2023–24 Saudi Pro League]] title; despite Neymar playing just three games in the league, he collected his eighth career league title.<ref name=":56" />

Neymar returned to action for [[2024–25 Al Hilal SFC season|2024–25]] on 21 October 2024 during Al Hilal's [[2024–25 AFC Champions League Elite|AFC Champions League]] match against [[Al Ain FC|Al Ain]].<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.beinsports.com/en-mena/football/afc-champions-league/articles-video/neymar-returns-for-al-hilal-in-al-ain-thriller-2024-10-21 |title=Neymar returns for Al Hilal in Al Ain thriller |publisher=beIN SPORTS |date=21 October 2024}}</ref> However, he soon suffered a hamstring injury that sidelined him for the rest of the year.<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.reuters.com/sports/soccer/neymar-miss-least-month-due-hamstring-injury-2024-11-06/ |title=Neymar to miss at least a month due to hamstring injury |publisher=Reuters |date=6 November 2024 }}</ref> According to Al Hilal coach [[Jorge Jesus]], Neymar could "no longer perform at the level we are used to" due to the lengthy injuries.<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.besoccer.com/new/neymar-not-registered-for-second-half-of-saudi-league-season-1344430 |title=Neymar NOT registered for second half of Saudi league season |publisher=BeSoccer |date=17 January 2025 }}</ref> On 27 January 2025, the club announced an agreement for the termination of Neymar's contract by mutual consent.<ref>{{Cite tweet |author=AlHilal Saudi Club |user=Alhilal_EN |number=1884015745589518834 |date=27 January 2025 |title=Full club statement 🔹 |access-date=27 January 2025 |link=https://x.com/Alhilal_EN/status/1884015745589518834 }}</ref> In a leaving message, Neymar said, "I gave it all to play, and I wish we could have enjoyed better moments together on the field".<ref>{{Cite web |last=play |first=The Brazilian posted a message on his social media directed at the team that has been his until now In the words, he assures that he gave it all to |title=Neymar's Farewell Message After Leaving Al-Hilal |url=https://www.beinsports.com/en-us/soccer/articles-video/neymar-s-farewell-message-after-leaving-al-hilal-2025-01-28 |access-date=12 July 2025 |website=beIN SPORTS |language=en-us}}</ref>

Neymar totalled one goal and two assists in seven appearances for Al Hilal;<ref>{{Cite web |date=28 January 2025 |title=Quanto Neymar ganhou no Al-Hilal e quantos jogos ele disputou pelo clube? |trans-title=How much did Neymar earn at Al Hilal, and how many matches did he play for the club? |url=https://www.estadao.com.br/esportes/quanto-neymar-ganhou-no-al-hilal-e-quantos-jogos-ele-disputou-pelo-clube/ |access-date= |website=Estadão |language=pt-br}}</ref> considering his transfer fee and wages, various media outlets debated whether Neymar's transfer to the club is the worst transfer in football history.<ref>{{Cite news |title=Who is football's worst transfer ever? From Neymar and Antony to Coutinho and Hazard |url=https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/6102516/2025/02/01/transfer-worst-ever-neymar-antony/# |access-date=2 March 2025 |work=The New York Times |date=February 2025 |issn=0362-4331}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=7 November 2024 |title=Neymar to Al-Hilal: The worst transfer in football history |url=https://www.goal.com/en-gb/lists/neymar-al-hilal-worst-transfer-football-history/bltb5ae92c60547f31d |access-date=2 March 2025 |website=Goal}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |author1=Ryan Dabbs |date=29 January 2025 |title=Is Neymar to Al-Hilal the worst transfer ever? |url=https://www.fourfourtwo.com/news/is-neymar-to-al-hilal-the-worst-transfer-ever |access-date=2 March 2025 |website=fourfourtwo.com}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |date=7 November 2024 |title=Neymar's Al Hilal Contract Situation Could Pave Way for Inter Miami Transfer |url=https://www.si.com/soccer/neymar-al-hilal-contract-inter-miami-transfer |access-date=28 February 2025 |website=SI }}</ref>

===Santos return===

==== 2025: Injuries, match-winning performances and avoiding relegation ====
On 30 January 2025, Neymar announced through his social media that he would be signing a contract with his first club [[Santos FC|Santos]].<ref>{{Cite web |date=28 January 2025 |title=Neymar returns to Brazilian club Santos after termination of contract in Saudi Arabia |url=https://apnews.com/article/saudi-al-hilal-neymar-terminate-contract-0c6470e475b427b6a240f0ec18e0bb48 |access-date=30 January 2025 |website=AP News }}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/sp/santos-e-regiao/futebol/times/santos/noticia/2025/01/30/neymar-anuncia-retorno-ao-santos.ghtml|title=Neymar anuncia retorno ao Brasil: "Só o Santos pode me proporcionar o carinho que eu preciso"|trans-title=Neymar announce return to Brazil: "Only Santos can give me the love that I need"|publisher=[[Grupo Globo|ge]]|language=pt-BR|date=30 January 2025|accessdate=30 January 2025}}</ref> The following day, Santos confirmed his return.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/sp/santos-e-regiao/futebol/times/santos/noticia/2025/01/31/santos-anuncia-a-contratacao-de-neymar.ghtml|title=Santos anuncia a contratação de Neymar: "Vai ser uma honra vestir esse manto sagrado"|trans-title=Santos announce the signing of Neymar: "It will be an honor to wear this holy shirt"|publisher=[[Grupo Globo|ge]]|language=pt-BR|date=31 January 2025|accessdate=31 January 2025}}</ref> One of the reasons for Neymar returning to Santos was to regain top form ahead of the [[2026 FIFA World Cup|2026 World Cup]].<ref>{{Cite web |title=Neymar on 2026 World Cup: 'I am going after this trophy in any way I can' |url=https://www.foxsports.com/stories/soccer/neymar-2026-world-cup-i-am-going-after-trophy-any-way-i-can |access-date=27 June 2025 |website=Fox Sports |language=en-US}}</ref> He started wearing the number 10 jersey, the number which Pelé had worn during his time spent between 1956 and 1974.<ref>{{Cite web |date=31 January 2025 |title=Foot : «Honorer ta légende», Neymar portera le mythique numéro 10 du roi Pelé avec Santos |url=https://www.lefigaro.fr/sports/football/foot-honorer-ta-legende-neymar-portera-le-mythique-numero-10-du-roi-pele-avec-santos-20250131 |access-date=2 February 2025 |website=Le Figaro |language=fr}}</ref>
Neymar made his re-debut for ''Peixe'' on 6 February, coming on as a half-time substitute for [[Gabriel Bontempo]] in a 1–1 Campeonato Paulista home draw against [[Botafogo Futebol Clube (SP)|Botafogo-SP]].<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.lance.com.br/santos/botafogo-surpreende-e-arranca-empate-com-o-santos-em-reestreia-de-neymar.html|title=Melhores momentos: Neymar reestreia com empate em Santos x Botafogo-SP|trans-title=Highlights: Neymar re-debuts with a draw at Santos vs Botafogo-SP|publisher=[[Lance!]]|language=pt-BR|date=5 February 2025|accessdate=6 February 2025}}</ref> According to [[Reuters]], "The Urbano Caldeira stadium erupted in celebration as jubilant and tearful fans lit up their mobile phones to give the striker, who left the club 12 years ago, a hero's welcome before kick-off."<ref>{{Cite web |title=Neymar makes lacklustre Santos return in 1–1 draw with Botafogo |url=https://www.reuters.com/sports/soccer/neymar-makes-lacklustre-santos-return-1-1-draw-with-botafogo-2025-02-06/ |publisher=[[Reuters]]|date=6 February 2025}}</ref> He received the [[Player of the match|Man of the Match]] award after the match.<ref>{{cite web |last1=Watach |first1=Nestor |title=Neymar's first touch on his fairytale Santos return is guaranteed to make you wince |url=https://www.planetfootball.com/latest/neymar-santos-debut-last-dance-era-moment-made-us-wince |website=Global Food |access-date=20 February 2025 |date=6 February 2025}}</ref>
[[File:Estádio Doutor Jorge Ismael de Biasi 2025 Novorizontino x Santos 10 (Neymar).jpg|thumb|150px|Neymar playing for [[Santos FC|Santos]] in February 2025]]

Neymar scored his first goal after returning ten days later, netting the opener in a 3–1 home win over [[Esporte Clube Água Santa|Água Santa]] through a penalty; he also assisted [[Guilherme (footballer, born 1995)|Guilherme]] in the club's third goal, and received another Man of the Match award.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/sp/santos-e-regiao/futebol/times/santos/noticia/2025/02/16/neymar-faz-primeiro-gol-em-retorno-ao-santos-e-volta-a-marcar-pelo-clube-apos-13-anos.ghtml|title=Neymar faz primeiro gol em retorno ao Santos e volta a marcar pelo clube após 13 anos|trans-title=Neymar scores first goal in return to Santos and scores for the club again after 13 years|publisher=[[Grupo Globo|ge]]|language=pt-BR|date=16 February 2025|accessdate=17 February 2025}}</ref> On 23 February, Neymar scored directly from a [[Olimpico goal|corner kick]] in a 3–0 win over [[Associação Atlética Internacional (Limeira)|Inter de Limeira]], and earned the Man of the Match award again.<ref>{{Cite web|url=https://www.upi.com/Sports_News/Soccer/2025/02/24/brazil-Neymar-first-corner-kick-goal/6901740406590/|title=Watch: Neymar irks jeering crowd by scoring first corner kick goal - UPI.com|website=United Press International}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/soccer/story/_/id/43992523/neymar-scores-directly-corner-draw-cheers-opposition-fans|title=Neymar scores direct from corner: 'I meant it'|date=24 February 2025|publisher=ESPN}}</ref> With his physical fitness experiencing an improvement compared to his time at Al-Hilal,<ref>{{Cite web |title=Neymar Shows Off Again with Santos as Rumors Grow About a Return to Barcelona |url=https://www.beinsports.com/en-us/soccer/articles-video/neymar-shows-off-again-with-santos-as-rumors-grow-about-a-return-to-barcelona-2025-03-03 |access-date=3 June 2025 |website=beIN SPORTS }}</ref> he would make his Série A debut on 13 March, coming on as a substitute 1–0 loss against [[Fluminense FC|Fluminense]]. In the same month, he was awarded the Best Goal of the [[2025 Campeonato Paulista]] for the direct corner kick goal in February.<ref name=":71" /> On 16 April, Neymar wore the number 100 jersey for the match against [[Clube Atlético Mineiro|Atlético Mineiro]] which resulted in a 2–0 win, landmarking his 100th match at the [[Estádio Urbano Caldeira|Vila Belmiro]]; he would feature in only 32 minutes during the match, however, and was subbed off after suffering an injury.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/sp/santos-e-regiao/futebol/times/santos/noticia/2025/04/16/neymar-usa-camisa-especial-em-comemoracao-ao-100o-jogo-pelo-santos-na-vila-veja.ghtml|title=Neymar usa camisa especial em comemoração ao 100º jogo pelo Santos na Vila; veja|trans-title=Neymar wears special shirt celebrating 100th match for Santos at the Vila; check out|publisher=[[Grupo Globo|ge]]|language=pt-BR|date=16 April 2025|accessdate=16 June 2025}}</ref>

Neymar spent more than a month sidelined before returning in [[2025 Copa do Brasil]] match against [[Clube de Regatas Brasil|CRB]] in [[Maceió]] on 22 May; coming on as a second-half substitute, he converted his penalty in the shoot-out, but his side was knocked out.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://exame.com/esporte/neymar-volta-apos-lesao-mas-santos-e-eliminado-pelo-crb-na-copa-do-brasil-2025/|title=Neymar volta após lesão, mas Santos é eliminado pelo CRB na Copa do Brasil 2025|trans-title=Neymar returns after injury, but Santos is knocked out by CRB in the 2025 Copa do Brasil|publisher=[[Exame]]|language=pt-BR|date=23 May 2025|accessdate=16 June 2025}}</ref> Neymar returned to the starting line-up on 1 June in a match against [[Botafogo de Futebol e Regatas|Botafogo]]. Deployed as a [[false nine]], he played for 76 minutes in the match before being sent off after [[Handball (association football)|handballing]]; Santos eventually lost the match 1–0, with the opponent's goal coming ten minutes after his ejection.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/sp/santos-e-regiao/futebol/times/santos/noticia/2025/06/01/neymar-e-expulso-apos-fazer-gol-de-mao-e-so-volta-a-jogar-pelo-santos-se-renovar-contrato.ghtml|title=Neymar é expulso após fazer gol de mão e só volta a jogar pelo Santos se renovar contrato|trans-title=Neymar is sent off after scoring hand goal and only returns to play for Santos if he renews his contract|publisher=[[Grupo Globo|ge]]|language=pt-BR|date=1 June 2025|accessdate=16 June 2025}}</ref> 

On 24 June, Santos announced Neymar's contract renewal until the end of the year, with an option for a further six months.<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.santosfc.com.br/eu-vou-eu-volto-eu-fico-onde-tudo-comecou-onde-nunca-vai-acabar-neymar-jr-renova-contrato-com-o-santos-fc/ |title="Eu vou, eu volto, eu fico. Onde tudo começou. Onde nunca vai acabar": Neymar Jr renova contrato com o Santos FC |trans-title="I go, I come back, I stay. Where it all began. Where it will never end": Neymar Jr renews contract with Santos FC |publisher=Santos FC |language=pt-BR |date=24 June 2025 |accessdate=25 June 2025}}</ref> The club's president Marcelo Teixeira said that Neymar's return to Santos has led to record-breaking social media growth and significant commercial gains for the club.<ref>{{Cite web |date=24 June 2025 |title=Neymar stays 'home,' renews Santos contract |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/45574408/neymar-extends-santos-contract-2025 |access-date=27 June 2025 |publisher=ESPN |language=en}}</ref> On his first match after the renewal, he scored the winner in a 1–0 home win over Flamengo on 16 July.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://english.news.cn/20250717/f04420f3847b4e098b37ccd47660c318/c.html|title=Neymar scores late as Santos beats Flamengo|agency=[[Xinhua News Agency]]|date=17 July 2025|accessdate=17 July 2025}}</ref>

On 4 August, Neymar scored twice in a 3–1 win against [[Esporte Clube Juventude|Juventude]], increasing Santos' hopes of remaining in the top division.<ref>{{Cite news |last=Sanderson |first=Tom |date=6 August 2025 |title=Neymar's return to Brazil: mixed form, a fight with a fan and a few great goals |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2025/aug/06/neymar-return-brazil-mixed-form-fight-fan-goals-santos |access-date=6 August 2025 |work=The Guardian |language=en-GB |issn=0261-3077}}</ref> In September, Santos coach [[Juan Pablo Vojvoda]] praised Neymar as "one of the greatest in history".<ref>{{Cite web |title=MSN |url=https://www.msn.com/en-us/sports/other/santos-coach-believes-neymar-is-one-of-the-greatest-in-history/vi-AA1MAB8y?cvid=68db8dddd22d44acb9971e7c39fd619a&ocid=sl |access-date=10 June 2026 |website=MSN}}</ref> In the same month, he suffered another [[thigh]] injury while training,<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.br/futebol/santos/artigo/_/id/15718793/neymar-registra-tratamento-lesao-ct-santos|title=Mais uma vez machucado, Neymar registra tratamento de lesão no CT do Santos|trans-title=Once again injured, Neymar registers treatment in Santos' training ground|publisher=[[ESPN Brasil]]|language=pt-BR|date=23 September 2025|accessdate=13 November 2025}}</ref> and spent 48 days sidelined before returning to action.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/sp/santos-e-regiao/futebol/times/santos/noticia/2025/11/01/neymar-volta-a-jogar-pelo-santos-apos-48-dias-fora-por-lesao-na-coxa.ghtml|title=Neymar volta a jogar pelo Santos após 48 dias fora por lesão na coxa|trans-title=Neymar returns to play for Santos after 48 days out due to a thigh injury|publisher=[[Grupo Globo|ge]]|language=pt-BR|date=1 November 2025|accessdate=13 November 2025}}</ref> Later that year, on 3 December, he scored his first hat-trick since his return in a 3–0 away win over Juventude, helping Santos to move out the relegation zone.<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/47195400/neymar-defies-injury-hat-trick-push-santos-relegation-zone |title=Neymar defies injury with hat trick to push Santos out of relegation zone |access-date=24 May 2026 |publisher=ESPN}}</ref><ref>{{cite web |url=https://onefootball.com/de/news/neymar-hat-trick-sends-santos-into-final-round-in-comfort-42044843 |title=Neymar hat-trick sends Santos into final round in comfort |publisher=OneFootball |date=4 December 2025 }}</ref> The goals took his goal tally for Santos to 150.<ref>{{Cite web |date=4 December 2025 |title=Neymar reaches incredible milestone of 150 goals for Santos: Where does he rank among club top goalscorers? |url=https://worldsoccertalk.com/news/neymar-reaches-incredible-milestone-of-150-goals-for-santos-where-does-he-rank-among-club-top-goalscorers/ |access-date=6 August 2026 |website=World Soccer Talk |language=en-US}}</ref>

==== 2026– ====
On 6 January 2026, Neymar extended his contract with Santos until the end of the year.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.santosfc.com.br/santos-fc-e-neymar-jr-renovam-contrato-ate-o-fim-de-2026/|title=Santos FC e Neymar Jr. renovam contrato até o fim de 2026|trans-title=Santos FC and Neymar Jr. renew contract until the end of 2026|publisher=Santos FC|language=pt-BR|date=6 January 2026|accessdate=7 January 2026}}</ref> On 26 February, he led Santos to a 2–1 victory over [[CR Vasco da Gama|Vasco da Gama]], scoring both goals.<ref>{{Cite web |date=27 February 2026 |title=Neymar shushes critics after first goals in Santos return |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/football/story/_/id/48048301/neymar-shushes-critics-first-goals-santos-return |access-date=6 August 2026 |website=www.espn.co.uk}}</ref> On 26 July, he scored twice against [[Associação Chapecoense de Futebol|Chapecoense]] in a 2–2 draw, scoring the equaliser from the penalty spot in the final minutes of the game.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Martín |first=Gloria |date=26 July 2026 |title=Neymar scored twice to earn Santos a draw in his return to the league |url=https://www.marca.com/en/football/2026/07/26/neymar-scored-twice-to-earn-santos-draw-in-his-return-to-the-league.html |access-date=6 August 2026 |website=MARCA |language=en-US}}</ref> On 4 August, Neymar assisted the only goal as Santos defeated [[Clube do Remo|Remo]] 1–0 in the [[2026 Copa do Brasil]] to progress to the quarter-finals, however, after the match tension was ignited between the two teams, with Neymar being captured on video taunting the staff of Remo by shouting "eliminated, eliminated".<ref name=":70">{{Cite web |title=Neymar called a 'lowlife,' accused of provoking players by rival club president |url=https://www.espn.com/soccer/story/_/id/49535948/neymar-called-lowlife-accused-provoking-players-rival-club-president |access-date=6 August 2026 |website=www.espn.com}}</ref> On 13 August, Neymar was decisive in the [[2026 Copa Sudamericana|Copa Sudamericana]] match against [[Club Deportivo Macará|Macará]], assisting both goals in a 2–1 comeback victory.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Fra |first=Amalia |date=2026-08-14 |title=Neymar sparks frenzy |url=https://en.as.com/soccer/neymar-sparks-frenzy-f202608-n/ |access-date=2026-08-14 |website=AS USA |language=en-us}}</ref>

On 2 September 2026, during a match against rivals [[SE Palmeiras|Palmeiras]] which sealed Santos' elimination from the national cup, Neymar suffered another thigh injury, being subbed off at half-time.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.br/futebol/santos/artigo/_/id/17203333/neymar-nova-lesao-muscular-sai-chorando-nem-volta-2%C2%BA-tempo-santos-x-palmeiras|title=Neymar sente nova lesão muscular, sai chorando e nem volta para 2º tempo de Santos x Palmeiras|trans-title=Neymar suffers a new muscle injury, leaves in tears, and does not return for the second half of the Santos v Palmeiras match|publisher=[[ESPN Brasil]]|language=pt-BR|date=2 September 2026|accessdate=21 September 2026}}</ref> He was also at the centre of controversy weeks later on 17 September; after being caught mocking the [[Clube Atlético Mineiro|Atlético Mineiro]] players after a 2–0 home win for the first leg of the Copa Sudamericana quarter-finals, he was himself a target of mockery after the club's elimination on penalties.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.terra.com.br/esportes/futebol/neymar-quebra-o-silencio-pela-primeira-vez-apos-eliminacao-do-santos,f7f71ebfc841b80665d8770e4d4e1262oy7td4jy.html|title=Neymar quebra o silêncio pela primeira vez após eliminação do Santos|trans-title=Neymar breaks his silence for the first time following Santos' elimination|publisher=[[Terra (company)|Terra]]|language=pt-BR|date=17 September 2026|accessdate=21 September 2026}}</ref> On 9 October, after nearly a month away due to injury, Neymar returned to action and rescued Santos from a loss against league leaders Flamengo, scoring two penalties after coming off the bench as his side drew 2–2.<ref>{{Cite web |date=2026-10-09 |title=On video: Neymar explodes in six minutes to rescue Santos from the Flamengo train {{!}} Goal.com |url=https://www.goal.com/en/news/on-video-neymar-explodes-in-six-minutes-to-save-santos-from-the-flamengo-train/blt4e150d32fb0398c9 |access-date=2026-10-09 |website=www.goal.com |language=en}}</ref> Late in that month, he received a yellow card (for throwing a ball in his opponent) and then a direct red card (for covering his mouth when talking) in a 1–1 home draw against [[Esporte Clube Bahia|Bahia]], both offences ocurring during altercations with Bahia's [[Rodrigo Nestor]]; he received a two-match ban (which was later reduced to a simple one-match suspension) for the red card incident, and another one-match ban for accumulation of yellow cards was also triggered.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/sp/santos-e-regiao/futebol/times/santos/noticia/2026/10/28/stjd-pune-neymar-do-santos-com-dois-jogos-de-suspensao.ghtml|title=STJD pune Neymar, do Santos, com dois jogos de suspensão por violar protocolo Vini Jr|trans-title=STJD hands Santos' Neymar a two-match suspension for violating the Vini Jr protocol|publisher=[[Grupo Globo|ge]]|language=pt-BR|date=28 October 2026|accessdate=29 October 2026}}</ref>

==International career==

=== 2010–2012: Senior debut and first major tournaments ===

[[File:Neymar.jpg|thumb|left|Neymar at a press conference for [[Brazil national football team|Brazil]] in August 2010. He made his senior debut that month aged 18.]]
Following Neymar's performances for Santos in their successful early 2010 season, including winning the [[2010 Copa do Brasil]], in which he was the top goalscorer, and the [[2010 Campeonato Paulista]], in which he scored 14 goals, former Brazilian football players Pelé and Romário reportedly urged coach [[Dunga]] to take Neymar to the [[2010 FIFA World Cup|2010 World Cup]].<ref name="Brazilians want Neymar">{{cite web |url=http://www.etaiwannews.com/etn/news_content.php?id=1228480&lang=eng_news&cate_img=145.jpg&cate_rss=news_Sports|title=Brazilians want young striker Neymar in World Cup|date=15 April 2010|work=Taiwan News|url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130825192428/http://www.etaiwannews.com/etn/news_content.php?id=1228480&lang=eng_news&cate_img=145.jpg&cate_rss=news_Sports|archive-date=25 August 2013}}</ref> Although the opinion that Neymar deserved a place in Dunga's squad went as far as a 14,000 signature petition,<ref name="Brazilians want Neymar"/><ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2010/may/04/world-cup-watch-37-days-to-go|title=World Cup watch ... 37 days to go|date=4 May 2010|work=The Guardian|location=UK|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170305032643/https://www.theguardian.com/football/2010/may/04/world-cup-watch-37-days-to-go|archive-date=5 March 2017}}</ref> and despite the pressure on Dunga to pick Neymar,<ref>{{cite news|url=https://news.bbc.co.uk/sport2/hi/football/world_cup_2010/8669086.stm|title=Brazil coach Dunga faces World Cup selection challenge|date=7 May 2010|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html|archive-date=11 June 2015}}</ref> he was omitted from both the squad of 23,<ref>{{cite news|url=https://news.bbc.co.uk/sport2/hi/football/world_cup_2010/8676077.stm|title=Ronaldinho left out of Brazil World Cup squad|date=11 May 2010|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170805150705/http://news.bbc.co.uk/sport1/hi/football/world_cup_2010/8676077.stm|archive-date=5 August 2017}}</ref><ref>{{cite news|url=http://sportsillustrated.cnn.com/2010/soccer/world-cup-2010/writers/tim_vickery/05/14/brazil.roster/|title=Brazil's Dunga unfazed by critics|date=18 May 2010|publisher=CNN|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20100521091201/http://sportsillustrated.cnn.com/2010/soccer/world-cup-2010/writers/tim_vickery/05/14/brazil.roster/|archive-date=21 May 2010}}</ref> and the stand-by list.<ref>{{cite web|url=http://pitacodogringo.wordpress.com/2010/05/11/tottenham%E2%80%99s-sandro-ranieri-on-brazil%E2%80%99s-world-cup2010-reserve-list/|title=Tottenham Hotspur's Sandro Ranieri on Brazil's World Cup2010 reserve list|date=11 May 2010|publisher=Picato do Gringo|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20100516235255/http://pitacodogringo.wordpress.com/2010/05/11/tottenham%E2%80%99s-sandro-ranieri-on-brazil%E2%80%99s-world-cup2010-reserve-list/|archive-date=16 May 2010}}</ref> Although Dunga described Neymar as "extremely talented", he said that the 18-year-old had not been tested sufficiently on the international level to earn a World Cup spot and he had failed to impress enough while on international duty.<ref>{{cite web|url=http://soccernet.espn.go.com/world-cup/story/_/id/785217/ce/uk/?cc=5739&ver=global |title=Ronaldinho misses Brazil cut |date=11 May 2010 |work=ESPN Soccernet |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20100514091654/http://soccernet.espn.go.com/world-cup/story/_/id/785217/ce/uk/?cc=5739&ver=global |archive-date=14 May 2010 }}</ref>

On 26 July 2010, Neymar was selected for the first time in the [[Brazil national football team|Brazil senior team]] by new head coach [[Mano Menezes]] for a friendly match against the [[United States men's national soccer team|United States]] to be played in [[East Rutherford, New Jersey]]. On 10 August 2010, he made his debut with the national team in that game, aged 18 years old, starting the match and wearing the number 11 jersey. He scored on his debut after 28 minutes, a header coming from an [[André Santos]] cross in a 2–0 win for Brazil.<ref>{{cite web|url=http://soccernet.espn.go.com/news/story?id=813452&sec=global&cc=5739|title=Neymar scores on Brazil debut|date=11 August 2010|work=ESPN Soccernet|access-date=13 August 2010|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121102085237/http://soccernet.espn.go.com/news/story?id=813452&sec=global&cc=5739|archive-date=2 November 2012}}</ref> On 1 March 2011, Neymar said: "Being on the Brazilian team is a privilege. There are some marvelous players and I'm very happy to be among them."<ref name="Samba Star" />
[[File:André Santos, Neymar and Ramires celebrate Neymars goal.jpg|thumb|right|Neymar [[Goal celebration|celebrates his goal]] for Brazil against [[Scotland national football team|Scotland]], on 27 March 2011, with [[André Santos]] and [[Ramires]].]]
On 27 March 2011, he scored twice in a 2–0 win against [[Scotland national football team|Scotland]] at the [[Emirates Stadium]].<ref>{{cite news|url=https://news.bbc.co.uk/sport2/hi/scotland/9431234.stm|title=Scotland 0–2 Brazil|date=27 March 2011|work=BBC Sport|access-date=29 March 2011|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html|archive-date=11 June 2015}}</ref> After he scored from the penalty spot, a banana was thrown onto the pitch, leading Neymar to complain about 'constant jeering and an atmosphere of racism' from the stands.<ref>{{cite news | url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/mar/27/neymar-racism-brazil-scotland | location=London | work=The Guardian | first=Ewan | last=Murray | title=Brazil's Neymar answers back after banana is thrown in Scotland match | date=27 March 2011 | url-status=live | archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170305031551/https://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/mar/27/neymar-racism-brazil-scotland | archive-date=5 March 2017}}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=http://www.mirrorfootball.co.uk/news/International-friendly-Scotland-0-2-Brazil-Neymar-complains-that-banana-was-thrown-at-him-article717728.html |title=Brazil-Scotland friendly marred by Neymar banana incident |date=27 March 2011 |work=BBC Sport |access-date=29 March 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110728094347/http://www.mirrorfootball.co.uk/news/International-friendly-Scotland-0-2-Brazil-Neymar-complains-that-banana-was-thrown-at-him-article717728.html |archive-date=28 July 2011 }}</ref> While Scottish officials explained that Neymar had been booed solely for perceived injury-feigning, a German student who was in the stadium amongst Brazil supporters said that he threw the banana with no racist intentions. This led to the [[Scottish Football Association]] asking the [[Brazilian Football Confederation]] for an apology for the accusations made to the Scottish fans.<ref>{{cite news | url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/mar/29/german-teenager-banana-brazil | location=London | work=The Guardian | first=Ewan | last=Murray | title=German teenager admits throwing banana during Brazil v Scotland match | date=29 March 2011 | url-status=live | archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170305032357/https://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/mar/29/german-teenager-banana-brazil | archive-date=5 March 2017}}</ref><ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/scotland/12925272|title=Scottish FA seeks apology from Brazil over racism claim|date=31 March 2011|work=BBC Sport|access-date=31 March 2011|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html|archive-date=11 June 2015}}</ref> Neymar refused to apologise or retract his words, claiming he "did not accuse any person or any group of supporters".<ref>{{cite news | url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/mar/31/scottish-fa-apology-brazil-racism-neymar | location=London | work=The Guardian | first=Ewan | last=Murray | title=Scottish FA demands apology from Brazil over claims of racism | date=31 March 2011 | url-status=live | archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170305032254/https://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/mar/31/scottish-fa-apology-brazil-racism-neymar | archive-date=5 March 2017}}</ref><ref>{{cite news | url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/apr/01/brazil-neymar-scotland-racism-claims | location=London | work=The Guardian | title=Brazil's Neymar refuses to apologise to Scotland for racism claims | date=1 April 2011 | url-status=live | archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170305031830/https://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/apr/01/brazil-neymar-scotland-racism-claims | archive-date=5 March 2017}}</ref>

Neymar was the leading goalscorer of the [[2011 South American Youth Championship]] with nine goals, including two in the final, in Brazil's 6–0 win against Uruguay.<ref>{{cite news|title=Brazil hit heights once more |url=https://www.fifa.com/u20worldcup/news/y=2011/m=2/news=brazil-hit-heights-once-more-1382702.html|publisher=FIFA|date=21 August 2016|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170305063735/http://www.fifa.com/u20worldcup/news/y%3D2011/m%3D2/news%3Dbrazil-hit-heights-once-more-1382702.html|archive-date=5 March 2017}}</ref> He also took part at the [[2011 Copa América]] in [[Argentina]], where he scored two goals in the first-round game against [[Ecuador national football team|Ecuador]]. He was selected 'Man of the Match' in Brazil's first match against [[Venezuela national football team|Venezuela]], which ended a [[2011 Copa América Group B#Brazil v Venezuela|1–1]] draw. Brazil were eliminated in the quarter-finals in a [[Penalty shoot-out (association football)|penalty shoot-out]] against [[Paraguay national football team|Paraguay]] (2–2 [[Overtime (association football)|a.e.t.]]), with Neymar being substituted in the 80th minute.<ref>{{cite news|title=Brazil v Paraguay – As it happened |url=https://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/jul/17/brazil-paraguay-live-copa-america|work=The Guardian|date=21 August 2016|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140810012754/http://www.theguardian.com/football/2011/jul/17/brazil-paraguay-live-copa-america|archive-date=10 August 2014}}</ref>
[[File:Lucas e Neymar 02.jpg|thumb|Neymar with Brazilian teammate [[Lucas Moura]] on international duty in 2012]]
By 20 years old, Neymar was viewed as the star player of the national team.<ref>{{Cite news |last=Borden |first=Sam |date=10 July 2012 |title=A Soccer Prodigy, at Home in Brazil |url=https://www.nytimes.com/2012/07/10/sports/soccer/neymar-soccer-prodigy-is-at-home-in-brazil.html |access-date=2 June 2025 |work=The New York Times |issn=0362-4331}}</ref> At this point, he was described as "a national demigod in Brazil" by ''[[SportsPro]]''.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Connolly |first=Eoin |date=17 May 2012 |title=50 Most Marketable 2012 – Neymar |url=https://www.sportspro.com/analysis/insights/analysis/50_most_marketable_2012_-_neymar/ |access-date=2 September 2026 |website=SportsPro}}</ref> On 11 May 2012, Neymar was selected for the squad of the [[Brazil Olympic football team]] to participate in the [[London 2012 Olympic Games]].<ref>{{cite news|url=http://www1.skysports.com/olympics/football/ones-to-watch/21746/7060689/ |location=London |publisher=Sky Sports |title=Neymar Profile for 2012 Olympics |date=29 June 2012 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120623115348/http://www1.skysports.com/olympics/football/ones-to-watch/21746/7060689/ |archive-date=23 June 2012 }}</ref>
In Brazil's first warm-up match on 20 July 2012, against the host nation [[Great Britain national football team|Great Britain]] at [[Riverside Stadium]], Neymar was involved in both goals of a 2–0 win, first making an assist with a free-kick into the six-yard box for [[Sandro (footballer, born 1989)|Sandro]]'s header, before converting a penalty.<ref>{{cite news | url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/olympics/18929315 | location=London | work=BBC Sport | title=Team GB were outclassed by Neymar | date=20 July 2012 | url-status=live | archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120904210333/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/0/olympics/18929315 | archive-date=4 September 2012}}</ref> On 26 July, he scored his first goal in Brazil's opening fixture against [[Egypt national under-23 football team|Egypt]], which ended a 3–2 victory for Brazil.<ref>{{cite news |url=http://london2012.bbc.co.uk/football/event/men/match=fbm400c02/doc=results.html |location=London |work=BBC Sport |title=Brazil 3–2 Egypt |date=26 July 2012 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120731223405/http://london2012.bbc.co.uk/football/event/men/match%3Dfbm400c02/doc%3Dresults.html |archive-date=31 July 2012 }}</ref> In the following match against [[Belarus national under-23 football team|Belarus]] at [[Old Trafford]] in Manchester, Neymar scored a free-kick from 25-yards into the top-right corner of the goal and set up goals for [[Alexandre Pato]]'s header with a cross from the right and [[Oscar (footballer, born 1991)|Oscar]] with a back heel as Brazil secured their place in the quarter-finals with a 3–1 win. Afterwards he said: "I scored and made two assists so for me it was perfect".<ref>{{cite news | url=http://soccernet.espn.go.com/news/story/_/id/1128175/men's-olympics:-neymar-lifts-brazil,-uruguay-stunned?cc=3888 | publisher=ESPN | title=Neymar lifts Brazil | date=29 July 2012 | url-status=dead | archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150611133541/http://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/newsid%3D2255077.html | archive-date=11 June 2015}}</ref><ref>{{cite news|url=http://london2012.bbc.co.uk/football/event/men/match=fbm400c03/index.html |location=London |work=BBC Sport |title=Brazil 3–1 Belarus |date=29 July 2012 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120730083726/http://london2012.bbc.co.uk/football/event/men/match%3Dfbm400c03/index.html |archive-date=30 July 2012 }}</ref>

On 5 August 2012, in the quarter-final encounter against [[Honduras national under-23 football team|Honduras]], Neymar scored a penalty, his third goal of the tournament and assisted in [[Leandro Damião]]'s second goal on the match, to help Brazil to a 3–2 victory at [[St James' Park]] and book a place in the semi-finals.<ref>{{cite news|url=http://london2012.bbc.co.uk/football/event/men/match=fbm400302/index.html |location=London |work=BBC Sport |title=Brazil 3–2 Honduras |date=4 August 2012 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120805013453/http://london2012.bbc.co.uk/football/event/men/match%3Dfbm400302/index.html |archive-date=5 August 2012 }}</ref> On 11 August, Brazil lost 2–1 to [[Mexico national under-23 football team|Mexico]] in the final at [[Wembley Stadium]] in London.<ref>{{cite news|title=Mexico 2–1 Brazil: Olympic final match report|url=https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/olympics/football/9469331/Mexico-2-1-Brazil-Olympic-final-match-report.html|issue=12 August 2012|work=The Telegraph|date=9 April 2015|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20151006211535/http://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/olympics/football/9469331/Mexico-2-1-Brazil-Olympic-final-match-report.html|archive-date=6 October 2015}}</ref>

Neymar scored his first full international hat-trick on 10 September 2012, in an 8–0 win over [[China national football team|China]] at the [[Estádio José do Rego Maciel]] in [[Recife]].<ref>{{cite news|last1=Edwards|first1=Daniel|title=Brazil 8–0 China: Neymar nets hat trick in crushing victory|url=http://www.goal.com/en-us/match/brazil-vs-china-pr/1377193/report?ICID=MP_MS_5|access-date=12 June 2014|work=Goal |date=10 September 2012|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140726091739/http://www.goal.com/en-us/match/brazil-vs-china-pr/1377193/report?ICID=MP_MS_5|archive-date=26 July 2014}}</ref> On 19 September, Neymar scored the winner against Argentina in a 2–1 win in the first leg of the [[2012 Superclásico de las Américas]] at the [[Estádio Serra Dourada]] in [[Goiânia]], Brazil.<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.fifa.com/worldfootball/news/newsid=1939984.html |title=Brazil edge rivals Argentina |date=22 November 2012 |publisher=FIFA |access-date=30 June 2014 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140709012021/http://www.fifa.com/worldfootball/news/newsid%3D1939984.html |archive-date=9 July 2014 }}</ref>

=== 2013–2014: Confederations Cup triumph and World Cup breakthrough ===
[[File:Pique&neymar.jpg|thumb|Neymar playing against Spain's [[Gerard Piqué]] in the [[2013 FIFA Confederations Cup Final]]]]

Neymar was selected as part of [[Luiz Felipe Scolari]]'s Brazil squad for the [[2013 FIFA Confederations Cup|2013 Confederations Cup]] on home soil. For the tournament he was assigned the number 10 shirt, having previously worn 11.<ref>{{Cite news|url=http://sports.inquirer.net/105255/football-neymar-renews-brazils-number-10-love-affair|title=Neymar renews Brazil's number 10 love affair|date=16 June 2013|access-date=18 June 2013|work=Inquirer|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130620162230/http://sports.inquirer.net/105255/football-neymar-renews-brazils-number-10-love-affair|archive-date=20 June 2013}}</ref>

Neymar scored the first goal of the tournament in a 3–0 win over [[Japan national football team|Japan]] at the [[Estádio Nacional Mané Garrincha]] on 15 June.<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.fifa.com/confederationscup/news/newsid=2109892/index.html |title=Three and easy for Brazil |publisher=FIFA |date=15 June 2013 |access-date=18 May 2014 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140522143826/http://www.fifa.com/confederationscup/news/newsid%3D2109892/index.html |archive-date=22 May 2014 }}</ref> In their second match, Neymar scored after nine minutes and crossed for [[Jô]]'s late goal to give Brazil a 2–0 win over [[Mexico national football team|Mexico]].<ref>{{cite web|url=http://sportsillustrated.cnn.com/soccer/news/20130619/brazil-mexico-neymar-confederations-cup.ap/ |title=Neymar, Brazil beat Mexico in Confederations Cup |agency=Associated Press |magazine=Sports Illustrated |date=19 June 2013 |access-date=18 May 2014 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131203004135/http://sportsillustrated.cnn.com/soccer/news/20130619/brazil-mexico-neymar-confederations-cup.ap/ |archive-date=3 December 2013 }}</ref> He scored in his third consecutive match, with a powerful free kick from the edge of the penalty area, as Brazil beat [[Italy national football team|Italy]] 4–2, and received his third consecutive man-of-the-match award.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.fifa.com/confederationscup/matches/round=255199/match=300222457/summary.html |title=Fred brace sees Brazil top group |publisher=FIFA |date=22 June 2013 |access-date=18 May 2014 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140522142913/http://www.fifa.com/confederationscup/matches/round%3D255199/match%3D300222457/summary.html |archive-date=22 May 2014 }}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.fifa.com/confederationscup/awards/manofthematch/ |title=Man of the match |publisher=FIFA |access-date=18 May 2014 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140522143319/http://www.fifa.com/confederationscup/awards/manofthematch/ |archive-date=22 May 2014 }}</ref> On 30 June, Neymar was involved in [[Fred (footballer, born 1983)|Fred]]'s opening goal and then subsequently scored Brazil's second in the 3–0 [[2013 FIFA Confederations Cup Final|final]] victory over [[Spain national football team|Spain]].<ref name="Brazil 3–0 Spain">{{cite web|author=Ben Smith|date=1 July 2013|title=Brazil 3–0 Spain|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/23093784|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20190808215622/https://www.bbc.com/sport/football/23093784|archive-date=8 August 2019|access-date=2 September 2017|work=BBC Sport}}</ref> Neymar's performances saw him receive the [[Golden Ball (FIFA Confederations Cup)|Golden Ball]] for player of the tournament.<ref name="prosoccertalk.nbcsports.com">{{cite news|url=http://prosoccertalk.nbcsports.com/2013/06/30/confederations-cup-awards-neymar-fernando-torres-brazil-spain-golden-ball-shoe-boot/|title=Neymar wins best player, and yes, Torres picks up another award|date=30 June 2013|work=NBC|url-status=live|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130702160231/http://prosoccertalk.nbcsports.com/2013/06/30/confederations-cup-awards-neymar-fernando-torres-brazil-spain-golden-ball-shoe-boot/|archive-date=2 July 2013}}</ref> He also finished in 5th place for the [[2013 FIFA Ballon d'Or]].<ref>{{Cite web |title=FIFA |url=http://www.fifa.com/mm/document/ballond%27or/general/02/26/14/03/votes_percentage_al_awards_neutral.pdf |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140712052118/http://www.fifa.com/mm/document/ballond%27or/general/02/26/14/03/votes_percentage_al_awards_neutral.pdf |archive-date=12 July 2014 |access-date=9 July 2025 |publisher=FIFA |url-status=live }}</ref>

On 5 March 2014, Neymar scored a hat-trick in a friendly win against [[South Africa national football team|South Africa]] in [[Johannesburg]]. He made headlines for his conduct after the final whistle when a South African boy ran onto the pitch. As security staff began to escort the boy from the field, Neymar intervened and introduced him to his Brazil teammates before they lifted him in the air during their celebrations.<ref name="South Africa">[https://www.mirror.co.uk/sport/football/news/watch-heartwarming-moment-neymar-brazil-3211567 "Watch the heartwarming moment Neymar and Brazil embraced a jubilant young South African fan"] {{webarchive|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140603195808/http://www.mirror.co.uk/sport/football/news/watch-heartwarming-moment-neymar-brazil-3211567 |date=3 June 2014 }}. The Mirror. Retrieved 3 June 2014</ref><ref>[http://ftw.usatoday.com/2014/03/neymar-brazil-south-africa-little-adorable-kid "An adorable kid ran on to the field during the Brazil-South Africa game"] {{webarchive|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140606204118/http://ftw.usatoday.com/2014/03/neymar-brazil-south-africa-little-adorable-kid |date=6 June 2014 }}. USA Today. Retrieved 3 June 2014</ref>
